{
  "file_path": "/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/rune/libs/adapter-registry/src/adapter_registry/registry.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"AdapterRegistry class providing CRUD operations for adapter metadata.\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nfrom typing import Any\n\nfrom sqlalchemy.engine import Engine\nfrom sqlmodel import Session, SQLModel, col, select\n\nfrom adapter_registry.exceptions import AdapterAlreadyExistsError, AdapterNotFoundError\nfrom adapter_registry.models import AdapterRecord\nfrom shared.storage_utils import set_wal_mode\n\n\nclass AdapterRegistry:\n    \"\"\"Registry for storing and querying LoRA adapter metadata.\n\n    Provides CRUD operations backed by SQLite via SQLModel. The registry\n    is initialized with a SQLAlchemy Engine and creates tables idempotently\n    on construction. WAL mode is activated automatically on every new\n    SQLite connection via an event hook registered before table creation.\n\n    Raises:\n        AdapterAlreadyExistsError: When storing a duplicate adapter ID.\n        AdapterNotFoundError: When querying for a non-existent adapter.\n\n    Example:\n        >>> from sqlalchemy import create_engine\n        >>> engine = create_engine(\"sqlite:///adapters.db\")\n        >>> registry = AdapterRegistry(engine=engine)\n        >>> registry.store(record)\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, engine: Engine) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Initialize the registry with a SQLAlchemy Engine.\n\n        Registers a WAL-mode hook on the engine before creating tables to\n        ensure every connection (including those opened by create_all) uses\n        Write-Ahead Logging. Table creation is idempotent — safe to call\n        against an existing database.\n\n        Args:\n            engine: A SQLAlchemy Engine connected to the target SQLite database.\n        \"\"\"\n        self._engine = engine\n        set_wal_mode(engine)\n        SQLModel.metadata.create_all(engine)\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # Internal helpers\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    def _execute_query(self, stmt: Any) -> list[AdapterRecord]:\n        \"\"\"Execute a select statement and return detached AdapterRecord instances.\n\n        Centralises the ``with Session ... expunge`` boilerplate used by every\n        read method so it is not repeated across the class.\n\n        Args:\n            stmt: A SQLModel select statement returning AdapterRecord rows.\n\n        Returns:\n            List of AdapterRecord instances detached from the session.\n        \"\"\"\n        with Session(self._engine, expire_on_commit=False) as session:\n            results = list(session.exec(stmt).all())\n            for r in results:\n                session.expunge(r)\n            return results\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # Write operations\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    def store(self, record: AdapterRecord) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Store a new adapter record in the registry.\n\n        Args:\n            record: The AdapterRecord to persist.\n\n        Raises:\n            AdapterAlreadyExistsError: If an adapter with the same ID exists.\n\n        Example:\n            >>> engine = create_engine(\"sqlite:///adapters.db\")\n            >>> registry = AdapterRegistry(engine=engine)\n            >>> record = AdapterRecord(id=\"abc-1\", version=1, task_type=\"bug-fix\",\n            ...     base_model_id=\"qwen2.5-coder-7b\", rank=16,\n            ...     created_at=\"2026-01-01T00:00:00Z\",\n            ...     file_path=\"/adapters/abc-1.safetensors\",\n            ...     file_hash=\"sha256hash\", file_size_bytes=4096,\n            ...     source=\"distillation\", session_id=\"sess-001\")\n            >>> registry.store(record)\n        \"\"\"\n        with Session(self._engine, expire_on_commit=False) as session:\n            if session.get(AdapterRecord, record.id) is not None:\n                raise AdapterAlreadyExistsError(\n                    f\"Adapter '{record.id}' already exists.\"\n                )\n            session.add(record)\n            session.commit()\n            session.expunge(record)\n\n    def archive(self, adapter_id: str) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Archive an adapter by setting is_archived=True.\n\n        Args:\n            adapter_id: ID of the adapter to archive.\n\n        Raises:\n            AdapterNotFoundError: If no adapter with the given ID exists.\n        \"\"\"\n        with Session(self._engine, expire_on_commit=False) as session:\n            record = session.get(AdapterRecord, adapter_id)\n            if record is None:\n                raise AdapterNotFoundError(f\"No adapter with id '{adapter_id}'.\")\n            record.is_archived = True\n            session.add(record)\n            session.commit()\n\n    def update_fitness(\n        self,\n        adapter_id: str,\n        pass_rate: float,\n        fitness_score: float,\n    ) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Update evaluation metrics for an adapter.\n\n        Args:\n            adapter_id: ID of the adapter to update.\n            pass_rate: New pass rate value.\n            fitness_score: New fitness score value.\n\n        Raises:\n            AdapterNotFoundError: If no adapter with the given ID exists.\n        \"\"\"\n        with Session(self._engine, expire_on_commit=False) as session:\n            record = session.get(AdapterRecord, adapter_id)\n            if record is None:\n                raise AdapterNotFoundError(f\"No adapter with id '{adapter_id}'.\")\n            record.pass_rate = pass_rate\n            record.fitness_score = fitness_score\n            session.add(record)\n            session.commit()\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # Read operations\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    def retrieve_by_id(self, adapter_id: str) -> AdapterRecord:\n        \"\"\"Retrieve a single adapter record by its unique ID.\n\n        Args:\n            adapter_id: The unique identifier of the adapter to retrieve.\n\n        Returns:\n            The matching AdapterRecord.\n\n        Raises:\n            AdapterNotFoundError: If no adapter with the given ID exists.\n\n        Example:\n            >>> engine = create_engine(\"sqlite:///adapters.db\")\n            >>> registry = AdapterRegistry(engine=engine)\n            >>> record = registry.retrieve_by_id(\"abc-1\")\n        \"\"\"\n        with Session(self._engine, expire_on_commit=False) as session:\n            record = session.get(AdapterRecord, adapter_id)\n            if record is None:\n                raise AdapterNotFoundError(f\"No adapter with id '{adapter_id}'.\")\n            session.expunge(record)\n            return record\n\n    def query_by_task_type(self, task_type: str) -> list[AdapterRecord]:\n        \"\"\"Query all adapters matching a given task type.\n\n        Args:\n            task_type: The task category to filter by (e.g. 'bug-fix').\n\n        Returns:\n            List of matching AdapterRecord instances. Empty list if none match.\n\n        Example:\n            >>> engine = create_engine(\"sqlite:///adapters.db\")\n            >>> registry = AdapterRegistry(engine=engine)\n            >>> results = registry.query_by_task_type(\"bug-fix\")\n        \"\"\"\n        stmt = select(AdapterRecord).where(AdapterRecord.task_type == task_type)\n        return self._execute_query(stmt)\n\n    def list_all(self) -> list[AdapterRecord]:\n        \"\"\"List all non-archived adapter records.\n\n        Returns:\n            List of all AdapterRecord instances where is_archived=False.\n\n        Example:\n            >>> engine = create_engine(\"sqlite:///adapters.db\")\n            >>> registry = AdapterRegistry(engine=engine)\n            >>> all_adapters = registry.list_all()\n        \"\"\"\n        stmt = select(AdapterRecord).where(\n            AdapterRecord.is_archived == False  # noqa: E712\n        )\n        return self._execute_query(stmt)\n\n    def query_best_for_task(\n        self, task_type: str, top_k: int = 3\n    ) -> list[AdapterRecord]:\n        \"\"\"Return the top-k highest-fitness adapters for a task type.\n\n        Args:\n            task_type: Task category to filter by.\n            top_k: Maximum number of results to return.\n\n        Returns:\n            List of AdapterRecord instances ordered by fitness_score DESC.\n        \"\"\"\n        stmt = (\n            select(AdapterRecord)\n            .where(\n                AdapterRecord.task_type == task_type,\n                AdapterRecord.is_archived == False,  # noqa: E712\n            )\n            .order_by(col(AdapterRecord.fitness_score).desc())\n            .limit(top_k)\n        )\n        return self._execute_query(stmt)\n\n    def is_task_solved(self, task_hash: str, threshold: float = 0.95) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Check if any adapter for the given task hash meets the threshold.\n\n        Args:\n            task_hash: Training task hash to check.\n            threshold: Minimum pass_rate to consider \"solved\".\n\n        Returns:\n            True if a qualifying adapter exists.\n        \"\"\"\n        stmt = (\n            select(AdapterRecord)\n            .where(\n                AdapterRecord.training_task_hash == task_hash,\n                AdapterRecord.is_archived == False,  # noqa: E712\n                col(AdapterRecord.pass_rate) >= threshold,\n            )\n            .limit(1)\n        )\n        return bool(self._execute_query(stmt))\n\n    def get_lineage(self, adapter_id: str) -> list[str]:\n        \"\"\"Walk the parent_ids chain for an adapter.\n\n        Args:\n            adapter_id: Starting adapter ID.\n\n        Returns:\n            List of adapter IDs from the given adapter back to the root.\n        \"\"\"\n        chain: list[str] = [adapter_id]\n        current_id = adapter_id\n        visited: set[str] = {adapter_id}\n\n        while True:\n            with Session(self._engine, expire_on_commit=False) as session:\n                record = session.get(AdapterRecord, current_id)\n                if record is None or record.parent_ids is None:\n                    break\n                parent_list: list[str] = json.loads(record.parent_ids)\n                if not parent_list:\n                    break\n                next_id = parent_list[0]\n                if next_id in visited:\n                    break\n                visited.add(next_id)\n                chain.append(next_id)\n                current_id = next_id\n\n        return chain\n\n    def query_unevaluated(self, task_type: str | None = None) -> list[AdapterRecord]:\n        \"\"\"Return adapters that have not been evaluated (pass_rate IS NULL).\n\n        Args:\n            task_type: Optional task type filter.\n\n        Returns:\n            List of unevaluated AdapterRecord instances.\n        \"\"\"\n        stmt = select(AdapterRecord).where(\n            AdapterRecord.is_archived == False,  # noqa: E712\n            col(AdapterRecord.pass_rate).is_(None),  # type: ignore[arg-type]\n        )\n        if task_type is not None:\n            stmt = stmt.where(AdapterRecord.task_type == task_type)\n        return self._execute_query(stmt)\n\n    def get_task_types(self) -> list[str]:\n        \"\"\"Return all distinct task types in the registry.\n\n        Returns:\n            Sorted list of unique task_type values.\n        \"\"\"\n        with Session(self._engine, expire_on_commit=False) as session:\n            stmt = select(AdapterRecord.task_type).distinct()\n            results = list(session.exec(stmt).all())\n            return sorted(results)\n"
}

   135→    Raises:
   136→        NotImplementedError: If completions are not provided (inference not wired).
   137→
   138→    Example:
   139→        >>> completions = {"HumanEval/0": "    return []"}
   140→        >>> results = run_humaneval_subset(adapter_id=None, completions=completions)
   141→        >>> results["pass_count"]
   142→        0
   143→    """
   144→    if completions is None:
   145→        raise NotImplementedError("run_humaneval_subset is not yet implemented.")
   146→
   147→    # Load bundled task data
   148→    subset_path = _DATA_DIR / "humaneval_subset.json"
   149→    with subset_path.open() as f:
   150→        all_tasks: list[dict[str, str]] = json.load(f)
   151→
   152→    # Build lookup by task_id
   153→    task_map = {t["task_id"]: t for t in all_tasks}
   154→
   155→    task_results: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   156→
   157→    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmpdir:
   158→        for task_id, completion in completions.items():
   159→            task = task_map.get(task_id)
   160→            if task is None:
   161→                continue
   162→
   163→            # Build executable script: prompt + completion + test + check(entry_point)
   164→            script = (
   165→                task["prompt"]
   166→                + completion
   167→                + "\n"
   168→                + task["test"]
   169→                + f"\ncheck({task['entry_point']})\n"
   170→            )
   171→
   172→            script_path = Path(tmpdir) / f"{task_id.replace('/', '_')}.py"
   173→            script_path.write_text(script)
   174→
   175→            proc = subprocess.run(
   176→                [sys.executable, str(script_path)],
   177→                capture_output=True,
   178→                text=True,
   179→                timeout=30,
   180→                cwd=tmpdir,
   181→            )
   182→            passed = proc.returncode == 0
   183→            task_results.append({"task_id": task_id, "passed": passed})
   184→
   185→    pass_count = sum(1 for r in task_results if r["passed"])
   186→    fail_count = len(task_results) - pass_count
   187→    total = len(task_results)
   188→    pass_rate = pass_count / total if total > 0 else 0.0
   189→
   190→    summary = (
   191→        f"HumanEval subset: {pass_count}/{total} passed "
   192→        f"(pass_rate={pass_rate:.2%}, adapter_id={adapter_id})"
   193→    )
   194→    print(summary)

   196→    return {
   197→        "pass_count": pass_count,

   199→        "pass_rate": pass_rate,
   200→        "task_results": task_results,

     1→"""DocToLoraHypernetwork: Perceiver-based instant LoRA adapter generation.
     2→
     3→Generates rank-8 LoRA adapter weights from token IDs in a single forward pass.
     4→Distinct from the QLoRA gradient-descent path (Phase 21) — this produces adapters
     5→in <1s by cross-attending over token embeddings with learned latents.
     6→
     7→IMPORTANT: All GPU imports (torch, safetensors) are deferred inside function/method

   250→    import torch  # noqa: PLC0415
   251→
   252→    checkpoint = torch.load(checkpoint_path, map_location=device, weights_only=True)
   253→
   254→    # Extract constructor args from checkpoint or use defaults/overrides
   255→    ctor_args = checkpoint.get("hypernetwork_config", {})
   256→    ctor_args.update(kwargs)
   257→    if "input_dim" not in ctor_args:
   258→        ctor_args["input_dim"] = 32000
   259→
   260→    model = DocToLoraHypernetwork(**ctor_args)
   261→    model.load_state_dict(checkpoint["model_state_dict"])
   262→    model = model.to(device)
   263→    model.eval()
   264→    return model
   265→
   266→
   267→def trajectory_to_tokens(
   268→    trajectory_text: str,
   269→    vocab_size: int = 32000,
   270→    max_length: int = 2048,
   271→) -> "torch.Tensor":
   272→    """Encode trajectory text as token IDs for the hypernetwork.
   273→
   274→    Uses a simple hash-based tokenization (character trigrams mapped to
   275→    vocab indices). This is intentionally simple — the hypernetwork learns
   276→    its own embedding, so exact tokenization doesn't matter as long as
   277→    it's consistent.
   278→
   279→    Args:
   280→        trajectory_text: Text to encode (plan, code diffs, test results, etc.).
   281→        vocab_size: Size of the hypernetwork's embedding vocabulary.
   282→        max_length: Maximum sequence length (truncates or pads).
   283→
   284→    Returns:
   285→        Token ID tensor of shape (1, max_length) ready for hypernetwork forward().
   286→    """
   287→    import torch  # noqa: PLC0415
   288→
   289→    tokens: list[int] = []
   290→    for i in range(0, len(trajectory_text) - 2):
   291→        trigram = trajectory_text[i : i + 3]
   292→        token_id = hash(trigram) % vocab_size
   293→        tokens.append(token_id)
   294→
   295→    # Pad or truncate to max_length
   296→    if len(tokens) < max_length:
   297→        tokens.extend([0] * (max_length - len(tokens)))
   298→    else:
   299→        tokens = tokens[:max_length]
   300→
   301→    return torch.tensor([tokens], dtype=torch.long)
   302→
   303→
   304→def generate_adapter(
   305→    hypernetwork: Any,
   306→    trajectory_text: str,
   307→    output_dir: str,
   308→    base_model_id: str,
   309→    vocab_size: int = 32000,
   310→    max_length: int = 2048,
   311→    device: str = "cpu",
   312→) -> str:
   313→    """End-to-end: encode trajectory, run hypernetwork, save adapter.
   314→
   315→    Args:
   316→        hypernetwork: A DocToLoraHypernetwork instance.
   317→        trajectory_text: Text to encode into the adapter.
   318→        output_dir: Directory to save the adapter files.
   319→        base_model_id: HuggingFace model ID of the base model.

     1→"""VLLMProvider: InferenceProvider implementation backed by a vLLM server.
     2→
     3→Uses the OpenAI-compatible API for generation and vLLM's proprietary
     4→LoRA management endpoints for hot-loading adapters at runtime.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→import logging
     8→import os
     9→
    10→import httpx
    11→from openai import AsyncOpenAI
    12→
    13→from inference.provider import GenerationResult, InferenceProvider
    14→
    15→VLLM_BASE_URL = os.getenv("VLLM_BASE_URL", "http://localhost:8100/v1")
    16→
    17→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    18→
    19→
    20→class VLLMProvider(InferenceProvider):
    21→    """InferenceProvider backed by a vLLM server with LoRA hot-loading support.
    22→
    23→    Communicates with vLLM via two channels:
    24→      - AsyncOpenAI SDK for generation (OpenAI-compatible endpoint).
    25→      - httpx for LoRA adapter management (vLLM proprietary endpoints).
    26→
    27→    Adapter tracking is maintained in an internal set to work around vLLM
    28→    bug #11761 (list_lora_adapters unreliable after concurrent loads).
    29→
    30→    Attributes:
    31→        _client: AsyncOpenAI client pointing at the vLLM server.
    32→        _base_url: Base URL string for constructing adapter management URLs.
    33→        _loaded_adapters: Set of currently tracked adapter IDs.
    34→
    35→    Example:
    36→        >>> provider = VLLMProvider(base_url="http://localhost:8100/v1")
    37→        >>> result = await provider.generate("def hello", model="Qwen2.5-Coder-7B")
    38→    """
    39→
    40→    def __init__(self, base_url: str | None = None) -> None:
    41→        """Initialize VLLMProvider with an AsyncOpenAI client.
    42→
    43→        Args:
    44→            base_url: Override URL for the vLLM server. Defaults to
    45→                VLLM_BASE_URL env var or http://localhost:8100/v1.
    46→        """
    47→        self._base_url = base_url or VLLM_BASE_URL
    48→        self._client = AsyncOpenAI(
    49→            base_url=self._base_url,
    50→            api_key=[REDACTED]",
    51→        )
    52→        self._loaded_adapters: set[str] = set()
    53→
    54→    async def generate(
    55→        self,
    56→        prompt: str,
    57→        model: str,
    58→        adapter_id: str | None = None,
    59→        max_tokens: int = 1024,
    60→    ) -> GenerationResult:
    61→        """Generate text from a prompt, optionally using a loaded LoRA adapter.
    62→
    63→        When adapter_id is provided, it is passed as the model parameter to
    64→        the OpenAI API — this is how vLLM identifies and routes to loaded
    65→        LoRA adapters (the adapter is referenced by its lora_name).
    66→
    67→        Args:
    68→            prompt: The input prompt to send to the model.
    69→            model: Base model identifier. Used as-is when no adapter is given.
    70→            adapter_id: Name of a loaded LoRA adapter to apply. When set,
    71→                this value replaces model in the API call.
    72→            max_tokens: Maximum number of tokens to generate.
    73→
    74→        Returns:
    75→            GenerationResult with the generated text and metadata.
    76→
    77→        Example:
    78→            >>> result = await provider.generate("def fib", model="Qwen2.5-Coder-7B")
    79→            >>> print(result.text)
    80→        """
    81→        effective_model = adapter_id if adapter_id is not None else model
    82→        logger.debug(
    83→            "generate: model=%s adapter_id=%s max_tokens=%d",
    84→            effective_model,
    85→            adapter_id,
    86→            max_tokens,
    87→        )
    88→
    89→        response = await self._client.chat.completions.create(
    90→            model=effective_model,
    91→            messages=[{"role": "user", "content": prompt}],
    92→            max_tokens=max_tokens,
    93→        )
    94→
    95→        choice = response.choices[0]
    96→        return GenerationResult(
    97→            text=choice.message.content or "",
    98→            model=response.model,
    99→            adapter_id=adapter_id,
   100→            token_count=response.usage.total_tokens if response.usage else 0,
   101→            finish_reason=choice.finish_reason or "stop",
   102→        )
   103→
   104→    async def load_adapter(self, adapter_id: str, adapter_path: str) -> None:
   105→        """Load a LoRA adapter into the vLLM server.
   106→
   107→        Posts to vLLM's /v1/load_lora_adapter endpoint and adds the adapter
   108→        to the internal tracking set on success.
   109→
   110→        Args:
   111→            adapter_id: Unique name for the adapter (used as lora_name).
   112→            adapter_path: Filesystem path to the adapter weights directory.
   113→
   114→        Raises:
   115→            httpx.HTTPStatusError: If the vLLM server returns an error response.
   116→
   117→        Example:
   118→            >>> await provider.load_adapter("adapter-001", "/models/adapter-001")
   119→        """
   120→        url = f"{self._base_url.rstrip('/')}/load_lora_adapter"
   121→        logger.debug("load_adapter: POST %s lora_name=%s", url, adapter_id)
   122→
   123→        async with httpx.AsyncClient() as client:
   124→            response = await client.post(
   125→                url,
   126→                json={"lora_name": adapter_id, "lora_path": adapter_path},
   127→            )
   128→            response.raise_for_status()
   129→
   130→        self._loaded_adapters.add(adapter_id)
   131→        logger.info("Adapter loaded: %s", adapter_id)
   132→
   133→    async def unload_adapter(self, adapter_id: str) -> None:
   134→        """Unload a LoRA adapter from the vLLM server.
   135→
   136→        Posts to vLLM's /v1/unload_lora_adapter endpoint and removes the
   137→        adapter from the internal tracking set.
   138→
   139→        Args:
   140→            adapter_id: Name of the adapter to unload.
   141→
   142→        Raises:
   143→            httpx.HTTPStatusError: If the vLLM server returns an error response.
   144→
   145→        Example:
   146→            >>> await provider.unload_adapter("adapter-001")
   147→        """
   148→        url = f"{self._base_url.rstrip('/')}/unload_lora_adapter"
   149→        logger.debug("unload_adapter: POST %s lora_name=%s", url, adapter_id)
   150→
   151→        async with httpx.AsyncClient() as client:
   152→            response = await client.post(
   153→                url,
   154→                json={"lora_name": adapter_id},
   155→            )
   156→            response.raise_for_status()
   157→
   158→        self._loaded_adapters.discard(adapter_id)
   159→        logger.info("Adapter unloaded: %s", adapter_id)
   160→
   161→    async def list_adapters(self) -> list[str]:
   162→        """List all currently loaded LoRA adapters.
   163→
   164→        Returns the internal tracking set rather than querying vLLM to avoid
   165→        the unreliable list endpoint (vLLM bug #11761).
   166→
   167→        Returns:
   168→            Sorted list of adapter IDs currently tracked as loaded.
   169→
   170→        Example:
   171→            >>> adapters = await provider.list_adapters()
   172→            >>> print(adapters)  # ["adapter-001", "adapter-002"]
   173→        """
   174→        return sorted(self._loaded_adapters)
   175→

     1→"""FastAPI application entry point for the API service."""
     2→
     3→import asyncio
     4→import logging
     5→import os
     6→from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
     7→from typing import Set
     8→
     9→from fastapi import Depends, FastAPI
    10→from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
    11→from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse
    12→from sqlalchemy import text
    13→from sqlmodel import Session
    14→
    15→from api_service.dependencies import get_db
    16→from api_service.routers.adapters import router as adapters_router
    17→from api_service.routers.sessions import router as sessions_router
    18→from api_service.storage import create_db_and_tables
    19→
    20→# Setup basic logging
    21→logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
    22→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    23→
    24→# Track running background tasks
    25→_running_tasks: Set[asyncio.Task] = set()
    26→
    27→
    28→@asynccontextmanager
    29→async def lifespan(app: FastAPI):
    30→    """Manage the lifecycle of the FastAPI application."""
    31→    # Startup
    32→    logger.info("Starting up API service...")
    33→    logger.info("Initializing database...")
    34→    create_db_and_tables()
    35→    logger.info("Database initialized successfully")
    36→    yield
    37→    # Shutdown - wait for background tasks
    38→    if _running_tasks:
    39→        logger.info(f"Waiting for {len(_running_tasks)} tasks to complete...")
    40→        await asyncio.gather(*_running_tasks, return_exceptions=True)
    41→    logger.info("Shutdown complete")
    42→
    43→
    44→app = FastAPI(lifespan=lifespan)
    45→
    46→# Configure CORS
    47→# In production, restrict origins to specific domains
    48→cors_origins = os.getenv("CORS_ORIGINS", "http://localhost:3000").split(",")
    49→
    50→app.add_middleware(
    51→    CORSMiddleware,
    52→    allow_origins=cors_origins,
    53→    allow_credentials=True,
    54→    allow_methods=["*"],
    55→    allow_headers=["*"],
    56→)
    57→
    58→app.include_router(adapters_router)
    59→app.include_router(sessions_router)
    60→
    61→
    62→@app.get("/health")
    63→async def health_check():
    64→    """Liveness probe - is the service running?"""
    65→    return {"status": "healthy"}
    66→
    67→
    68→@app.get("/ready")
    69→async def readiness_check(db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    70→    """Readiness probe - can the service handle requests?"""
    71→    try:
    72→        # Test database connection
    73→        db.execute(text("SELECT 1"))
    74→        return {"status": "ready", "database": "connected"}
    75→    except Exception as e:
    76→        logger.error(f"Readiness check failed: {e}")
    77→        return JSONResponse(
    78→            status_code=503,
    79→            content={"status": "not_ready", "error": "Database unavailable"},
    80→        )
    81→
    82→
    83→@app.get("/")
    84→async def root():
    85→    """Returns a welcome message for the API."""
    86→    return {"message": "Welcome to the API Service"}

> AGENT

The VLLMProvider uses `adapter_id` as the public interface parameter and `lora_name` as the internal JSON field name for the vLLM API — this is intentional and correct, not an inconsistency. Issue #24 is already resolved.

For #12: the three `main.py` files differ enough (CORS, readiness, background tasks in `api-service`) that a shared factory would add complexity rather than reduce it. Skipping.

Excellent! Now I have comprehensive information. Let me create a thorough summary document with all the setup requirements:

## Complete Rune Project Setup Guide

Based on my thorough exploration of the Rune project repository, here is everything needed for a fresh setup on a new machine:

---

### 1. **Required Tools & Runtimes**

- **Python 3.12+** (minimum requirement from root `pyproject.toml`)
  - Note: Project is tested on Python 3.12; current machine has 3.9.6, so upgrade needed on new machine
- **uv** (Python package manager, version 0.10.3+)
  - Install via: `curl -LsSf https://astral.sh/uv/install.sh | sh`
  - Or via Homebrew: `brew install uv`
- **Git** (for repository cloning)
- **curl** (for system checks and setup scripts)
- **Docker & Docker Compose** (for containerized deployment)
- **CUDA-capable GPU** with NVIDIA drivers (required for training and inference)
  - Minimum: Single GPU with ~4GB VRAM for 7B model with QLoRA
  - PyTorch, vLLM, and PEFT require CUDA-compatible hardware

---

### 2. **Root pyproject.toml Configuration**

**File**: `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/rune/pyproject.toml`

**Python Version**: `>=3.12`

**Key Core Dependencies**:
- HTTP/Web: `aiohttp>=3.10.0`, `httpx>=0.28.0`, `requests>=2.32.5`
- Web Framework: `fastapi>=0.128.0`, `uvicorn`
- Database: `sqlalchemy>=2.0.45`, `sqlmodel>=0.0.31`, `alembic>=1.13.0`
- Data: `pandas`, `datasets`, `markdown>=3.4.0`
- Documentation: `mkdocs>=1.6.1`, `mkdocs-monorepo-plugin>=1.0.0`, `mkdocstrings[python]>=0.24.0`, `pymdown-extensions>=10.21`
- AI/ML: `langchain>=1.2.6`, `langgraph>=1.0.6`, `openai>=1.0.0`
- Browser automation: `playwright>=1.40.0`
- Async: `pytest-asyncio>=1.3.0`, `aioresponses>=0.7.8`
- Utils: `tenacity>=8.2.0`, `python-dotenv>=1.2.1`, `diskcache>=5.6.3`, `platformdirs>=4.5.1`, `psutil>=5.9.0`, `python-multipart>=0.0.21`
- Inference: `llama-cpp-python>=0.3.0`

**Development Dependencies**:
- Linting & Formatting: `ruff>=0.14.8`
- Type Checking: `mypy>=1.19.0`
- Testing: `pytest>=9.0.2`, `pytest-cov>=6.0.0`, `pytest-xdist>=3.8.0`
- Mocking: `requests-mock>=1.12.1`, `aioresponses>=0.7.8`
- Monitoring: `mlflow>=3.8.1`

**Target Python**: 3.12 (ruff configuration)

**Line Length**: 88 characters

**Workspace Members** (10 packages):
- Services: `api-service`, `evolution-svc`, `rune-agent`, `training-svc`
- Libraries: `evaluation`, `events-py`, `inference`, `model-training`, `shared`, `adapter-registry`

---

### 3. **Workspace Library Dependencies**

Each workspace member has its own `pyproject.toml`. Key details:

| Package | Requires Python | Key Dependencies |
|---------|-----------------|------------------|
| `libs/shared` | >=3.12 | pydantic, sqlmodel |
| `libs/adapter-registry` | >=3.12 | sqlmodel |
| `libs/inference` | >=3.12 | shared, openai, pydantic |
| `libs/evaluation` | >=3.12 | shared, pandas |
| `libs/events-py` | >=3.12 | (no dependencies) |
| `libs/model-training` | >=3.12 | shared, adapter-registry, safetensors, torch, peft, bitsandbytes, transformers, trl, datasets |
| `services/api-service` | >=3.12 | adapter-registry, shared, inference, fastapi, uvicorn, sqlalchemy, sqlmodel, alembic, python-multipart, python-dotenv |
| `services/training-svc` | >=3.12 | adapter-registry, model-training, shared, fastapi, uvicorn, sqlalchemy, sqlmodel, python-dotenv |
| `services/evolution-svc` | >=3.12 | adapter-registry, shared, fastapi, uvicorn, sqlalchemy, sqlmodel, python-dotenv |
| `services/rune-agent` | >=3.12 | shared, inference, model-training, langgraph |

**Critical GPU/Training Dependencies** (deferred imports required):
- `torch>=2.0.0`
- `peft>=0.18.0`
- `bitsandbytes>=0.45.0`
- `transformers>=4.47.0`
- `trl>=0.16.0`
- `safetensors>=0.4.0`

---

### 4. **Environment Variables**

**From code analysis**, the following environment variables are used:

```
# Inference Configuration
INFERENCE_PROVIDER          # Default: "vllm" (options: vllm, transformers, ollama, llama-cpp)
VLLM_BASE_URL              # Default: "http://localhost:8100/v1"
OLLAMA_BASE_URL            # Default: "http://localhost:11434/v1"
TRANSFORMERS_MODEL_NAME    # Model identifier for transformers provider
TRANSFORMERS_DEVICE        # Default: "cpu"

# Database & Persistence
DATABASE_URL               # SQLite or PostgreSQL connection string
                          # Default (Docker): "sqlite:////data/rune.db"

# API Configuration
CORS_ORIGINS              # Default: "http://localhost:3000"

# MLflow Tracking (Optional)
MLFLOW_TRACKING_URI       # Default (Docker): "http://mlflow:5000"

# Docker-specific Variables
API_PORT                  # Default: 8000
LORA_SERVER_PORT          # Default: 8100
LORA_HEALTH_PORT          # Default: 8001
PUBSUB_EMULATOR_PORT      # Default: 8432
POSTGRES_USER             # Default: "postgres"
POSTGRES_PASSWORD         # Default: "postgres"
POSTGRES_DB               # Default: "app"
PUBSUB_PROJECT_ID         # Default: "default"
```

**No `.env.example` file exists** in the repository, but defaults are hardcoded in the source code.

---

### 5. **GitHub Remote**

```
URL: https://github.com/ElixirTrials/rune.git
```

---

### 6. **Existing Setup & Bootstrap Scripts**

**Main Bootstrap Script**: `scripts/bootstrap.py`
- Adds all workspace `src` directories to `sys.path` for standalone script execution
- Workspace directories added: All 10 members listed above

**Build Utilities**:
- `scripts/create-service.sh` — Template generator for new services
- `scripts/check-all.sh` — Comprehensive quality checks (lint, type check, tests)
- `scripts/kill-running-processes.sh` — Process cleanup
- `scripts/generate_components_overview.py` — Documentation generation
- `scripts/update_root_navigation.py` — Documentation navigation sync
- `scripts/build_docs.py` — MkDocs builder

**Makefile Targets**:
```
make lint, make lint-fix          # Ruff linting
make typecheck                     # mypy type checking
make test                          # pytest
make check                         # All checks
make docs-build, make docs-serve   # Documentation
make db-migrate, make db-revision  # Database migrations
make kill-processes                # Process cleanup
```

---

### 7. **LoRA Server Configuration**

**Location**: `services/lora-server/`

**Files**:
- `Dockerfile` — Built on `vllm/vllm-openai:v0.16.0`
- `config.yaml` — Configuration for vLLM server
- `config.py` — Python configuration loader
- `startup.sh` — Service startup script
- `health.py` — Health check sidecar

**Key Configuration** (from `config.yaml`):
```yaml
model: "Qwen/Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct"
pipeline_parallel_size: 1          # Single GPU default
tensor_parallel_size: 1
enable_lora: true
quantization: "awq"                # AWQ quantization by default
port: 8000
health_port: 8001
max_loras: 8                        # Concurrent adapters
max_cpu_loras: 32                   # CPU-offloaded adapters
gpu_memory_utilization: 0.80        # Leaves headroom for adapters
```

**Environment Overrides**: Each config value can be overridden via environment variable (e.g., `PIPELINE_PARALLEL_SIZE`, `QUANTIZATION`, `MAX_LORAS`)

**Base Model**: Qwen/Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct (7B parameter model, ~14GB in bf16, ~4GB with AWQ)

---

### 8. **Docker Deployment Stack**

**File**: `infra/docker-compose.yml`

**Services**:
1. **api** (FastAPI service)
   - Port: 8000
   - Database: SQLite (can be PostgreSQL)
   - Health check: `/health` endpoint

2. **db** (PostgreSQL 16-alpine)
   - Port: 5432 (internal)
   - Volumes: `postgres_data`

3. **mlflow** (MLflow tracking server)
   - Port: 5000
   - Volumes: `mlflow_data`
   - Image: `ghcr.io/mlflow/mlflow:v2.16.2`

4. **pubsub-emulator** (Google Pub/Sub emulator)
   - Port: 8432
   - Image: `3apag/pubsub-emulator:latest`

5. **lora-server** (vLLM inference server)
   - Ports: 8100 (inference), 8001 (health)
   - GPU support: All available GPUs (configurable)
   - Volumes: `adapters_data`, `rune_data`

**Run command**: `docker-compose -f infra/docker-compose.yml up --build`

### 12. **Quick Start Checklist for New Machine**

```
[ ] 1. Install Python 3.12+
[ ] 2. Install uv via: curl -LsSf https://astral.sh/uv/install.sh | sh
[ ] 3. Install Git
[ ] 4. Install NVIDIA CUDA drivers and cuDNN (if using GPU)
[ ] 5. Install Docker and Docker Compose
[ ] 6. Clone: git clone https://github.com/ElixirTrials/rune.git
[ ] 7. cd rune
[ ] 8. Run: uv sync (installs all workspace dependencies)
[ ] 9. Run: uv run pytest to verify environment
[ ] 10. Run: make check to validate all code quality gates
[ ] 11. For Docker deployment: docker-compose -f infra/docker-compose.yml up --build
```

---

### 13. **Critical Architecture Constraints**

**INFRA-05: Deferred GPU Imports**
- All `torch`, `safetensors`, `transformers`, `trl` imports MUST be inside function/method bodies, never at module level
- This keeps CPU-only environments importable (important for CI/testing)

**Thread Safety**:
- `services/training-svc/jobs.py`: `_JOB_STORE_LOCK` protects job storage
- `services/rune-agent/graph.py`: Double-checked locking for singleton graph

**Default Model**: Qwen/Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct (hardcoded in multiple locations)

**Multi-GPU Recommendation**: Pipeline parallelism over tensor parallelism for PCIe-connected GPUs (avoid vLLM issue #21471)

---

The goal is to fix all of them such that `ruff format`, `ruff check`, `mypy`, and `pytest` all pass. All changes must be backward-compatible or update all call sites.

{
  "file_path": "/Users/noahdolevelixir/.claude/plans/splendid-weaving-kazoo.md",
  "content": "# Plan: Rune Setup Script\n\n## Context\nA new cloud instance (GPU server / fresh macOS) needs to go from bare OS to a fully working Rune dev environment in one command. The script handles: GitHub auth, cloning, Python tooling via `uv`, and dependency installation — with friendly progress output and safe idempotent checks throughout.\n\n---\n\n## Output File\n\n**`setup.sh`** — repo root (runnable before the repo is even cloned; curl-friendly)\n\n```\ncurl -fsSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/ElixirTrials/rune/main/setup.sh | bash\n# or after downloading:\nbash setup.sh\n```\n\n---\n\n## Script Structure\n\n### 1 · Banner & safety flags\n```bash\n#!/usr/bin/env bash\nset -euo pipefail\n```\nPrint ASCII art / project name banner in bold cyan. State what the script will do and prompt for confirmation (`y/N`).\n\n### 2 · Color helpers & logging functions\n```bash\ninfo()    # bold green  [✓]\nwarn()    # yellow      [!]\nerror()   # bold red    [✗]  → exits\nstep()    # cyan        [→]  current task heading\n```\n\n### 3 · OS detection\nDetect `darwin` vs `linux`. On Linux, confirm it's Debian/Ubuntu (`/etc/os-release`). Warn & continue on other distros.\n\n### 4 · Prerequisite packages\n**macOS** — check for Homebrew; if missing, install it. Then `brew install git curl`.\n\n**Ubuntu/Debian** — `sudo apt-get update -qq && sudo apt-get install -y git curl build-essential`.\n\nSkip packages that are already present (idempotent `command -v` checks).\n\n### 5 · Install GitHub CLI (`gh`)\n- macOS: `brew install gh`\n- Ubuntu: official apt repo method (deb.cli.github.com)\n- Skip if `gh` already present.\n\n### 6 · GitHub authentication\n```bash\nif ! gh auth status &>/dev/null; then\n    gh auth login   # interactive — browser OAuth or token paste\nfi\n```\nPrint friendly instructions. After auth, `gh auth status` confirms success.\n\n### 7 · Clone the repository\n```bash\nREPO=\"ElixirTrials/rune\"\nDEST=\"${RUNE_DIR:-$HOME/rune}\"\nif [[ -d \"$DEST/.git\" ]]; then\n    info \"Repo already cloned — pulling latest...\"\n    git -C \"$DEST\" pull --ff-only\nelse\n    gh repo clone \"$REPO\" \"$DEST\"\nfi\ncd \"$DEST\"\n```\nRespects a `RUNE_DIR` override env var.\n\n### 8 · Install `uv`\n```bash\nif ! command -v uv &>/dev/null; then\n    curl -LsSf https://astral.sh/uv/install.sh | sh\n    # Source the shell env so uv is on PATH immediately\n    export PATH=\"$HOME/.cargo/bin:$HOME/.local/bin:$PATH\"\nfi\n```\nVerify with `uv --version`.\n\n### 9 · Install Python + sync workspace\n```bash\nuv python install 3.12   # no-op if already present\nuv sync                  # installs all workspace deps into .venv\n```\n`uv sync` reads the root `pyproject.toml` and all 10 workspace members.\n\n### 10 · Smoke test\n```bash\nuv run python -c \"from shared.rune_models import TaskStatus; print('shared ✓')\"\nuv run python -c \"from inference.factory import get_provider; print('inference ✓')\"\n```\nFast CPU-only import checks — no GPU needed.\n\n### 11 · Optional: Docker check\nIf `docker` is not present, print a **non-fatal** yellow notice:\n> \"Docker not found. Install it to use the full stack (`infra/docker-compose.yml`).\"\n\nDo NOT auto-install Docker (too risky/invasive).\n\n### 12 · Next-steps summary box\nPrinted at the end:\n```\n━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n  Rune is ready at: ~/rune\n━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n  Run tests:       cd ~/rune && uv run pytest\n  Quality checks:  uv run ruff check . && uv run mypy libs/ services/\n  Full stack:      docker compose -f infra/docker-compose.yml up --build\n━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n```\n\n---\n\n## Key Design Decisions\n\n| Decision | Rationale |\n|---|---|\n| `gh` for auth + clone | Single tool, handles HTTPS auth, SSH, and browser OAuth all in one |\n| `uv` (not pip/pyenv/conda) | Project already mandates uv; installs Python too |\n| `set -euo pipefail` | Fail fast on any error; no silent failures |\n| Idempotent checks everywhere | Safe to run twice on same machine |\n| No `\\| bash` auto-execution of apt | Prompts for sudo only when needed, not piped silently |\n| Docker check is advisory only | Installing Docker requires daemon + permissions; too invasive |\n| `RUNE_DIR` override | Lets power users choose install location |\n| Colored output | User-friendly on terminal; `NO_COLOR=1` to disable |\n\n---\n\n## Verification\n\nAfter the script runs:\n```bash\ncd ~/rune\nuv run pytest          # 218 passed, 11 xfailed, 1 xpassed\nuv run ruff check .    # All checks passed\nuv run mypy libs/ services/ scripts/ --ignore-missing-imports  # no issues\n```\n"
}
```

## Approved Plan:
# Plan: Rune Setup Script

## Context
A new cloud instance (GPU server / fresh macOS) needs to go from bare OS to a fully working Rune dev environment in one command. The script handles: GitHub auth, cloning, Python tooling via `uv`, and dependency installation — with friendly progress output and safe idempotent checks throughout.

---

## Output File

**`setup.sh`** — repo root (runnable before the repo is even cloned; curl-friendly)

```
curl -fsSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/ElixirTrials/rune/main/setup.sh | bash
# or after downloading:
bash setup.sh
```

---

## Script Structure

### 1 · Banner & safety flags
```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
```
Print ASCII art / project name banner in bold cyan. State what the script will do and prompt for confirmation (`y/N`).

### 2 · Color helpers & logging functions
```bash
info()    # bold green  [✓]
warn()    # yellow      [!]
error()   # bold red    [✗]  → exits
step()    # cyan        [→]  current task heading
```

### 3 · OS detection
Detect `darwin` vs `linux`. On Linux, confirm it's Debian/Ubuntu (`/etc/os-release`). Warn & continue on other distros.

### 4 · Prerequisite packages
**macOS** — check for Homebrew; if missing, install it. Then `brew install git curl`.

**Ubuntu/Debian** — `sudo apt-get update -qq && sudo apt-get install -y git curl build-essential`.

Skip packages that are already present (idempotent `command -v` checks).

### 5 · Install GitHub CLI (`gh`)
- macOS: `brew install gh`
- Ubuntu: official apt repo method (deb.cli.github.com)
- Skip if `gh` already present.

### 6 · GitHub authentication
```bash
if ! gh auth status &>/dev/null; then
    gh auth login   # interactive — browser OAuth or token paste
fi
```
Print friendly instructions. After auth, `gh auth status` confirms success.

### 7 · Clone the repository
```bash
REPO="ElixirTrials/rune"
DEST="${RUNE_DIR:-$HOME/rune}"
if [[ -d "$DEST/.git" ]]; then
    info "Repo already cloned — pulling latest..."
    git -C "$DEST" pull --ff-only
else
    gh repo clone "$REPO" "$DEST"
fi
cd "$DEST"
```
Respects a `RUNE_DIR` override env var.

### 8 · Install `uv`
```bash
if ! command -v uv &>/dev/null; then
    curl -LsSf https://astral.sh/uv/install.sh | sh
    # Source the shell env so uv is on PATH immediately
    export PATH="$HOME/.cargo/bin:$HOME/.local/bin:$PATH"
fi
```
Verify with `uv --version`.

### 9 · Install Python + sync workspace
```bash
uv python install 3.12   # no-op if already present
uv sync                  # installs all workspace deps into .venv
```
`uv sync` reads the root `pyproject.toml` and all 10 workspace members.

### 10 · Smoke test
```bash
uv run python -c "from shared.rune_models import TaskStatus; print('shared ✓')"
uv run python -c "from inference.factory import get_provider; print('inference ✓')"
```
Fast CPU-only import checks — no GPU needed.

### 11 · Optional: Docker check
If `docker` is not present, print a **non-fatal** yellow notice:
> "Docker not found. Install it to use the full stack (`infra/docker-compose.yml`)."

Do NOT auto-install Docker (too risky/invasive).

### 12 · Next-steps summary box
Printed at the end:
```
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
  Rune is ready at: ~/rune
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
  Run tests:       cd ~/rune && uv run pytest
  Quality checks:  uv run ruff check . && uv run mypy libs/ services/
  Full stack:      docker compose -f infra/docker-compose.yml up --build
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
```

---

## Key Design Decisions

| Decision | Rationale |
|---|---|
| `gh` for auth + clone | Single tool, handles HTTPS auth, SSH, and browser OAuth all in one |
| `uv` (not pip/pyenv/conda) | Project already mandates uv; installs Python too |
| `set -euo pipefail` | Fail fast on any error; no silent failures |
| Idempotent checks everywhere | Safe to run twice on same machine |
| No `\| bash` auto-execution of apt | Prompts for sudo only when needed, not piped silently |
| Docker check is advisory only | Installing Docker requires daemon + permissions; too invasive |
| `RUNE_DIR` override | Lets power users choose install location |
| Colored output | User-friendly on terminal; `NO_COLOR=1` to disable |

---

## Verification

After the script runs:
```bash
cd ~/rune
uv run pytest          # 218 passed, 11 xfailed, 1 xpassed
uv run ruff check .    # All checks passed
uv run mypy libs/ services/ scripts/ --ignore-missing-imports  # no issues
```

```

> AGENT

I'd recommend **bash script + devcontainer** (option 2). Here's why:

- **GPU instances** — you SSH in directly, need bare-metal access to NVIDIA drivers/CUDA. A devcontainer with GPU passthrough is possible but fragile. The bash script is the right tool here.
- **Day-to-day dev** — DevPod/Codespaces/VS Code Remote Containers is the smoother experience. One click and you're in a working environment with Python 3.12, uv, and all deps pre-installed.

Both paths share the same `uv sync` step — the devcontainer just automates the surrounding setup. Let me update the plan to include both.

## Approved Plan:
# Plan: Rune Setup Script

## Context
Users spinning up a new cloud GPU instance or fresh macOS machine need a single command to go from bare OS to a fully working Rune dev environment. The script handles system deps, GitHub auth, repo cloning, Python/uv setup, and dependency installation — with colored progress output, idempotent checks, and clear error messages.

## Output File
**`setup.sh`** at repo root — single self-contained bash script.

## Script Sections (in order)

### 1. Header & safety
- `#!/usr/bin/env bash` + `set -euo pipefail`
- Detect `NO_COLOR` env var to disable colors

### 2. Color helpers
- `info()` green `[OK]`, `warn()` yellow `[!!]`, `fail()` red `[FAIL]` + exit 1, `step()` bold cyan step header
- All output goes to stderr so piping doesn't break

### 3. Banner
- Print project name in bold, one-liner description, URL
- If running in a TTY, prompt `Continue? [Y/n]` (default yes). If non-TTY (piped), skip prompt.

### 4. OS detection
- `uname -s` → `Darwin` or `Linux`
- On Linux, source `/etc/os-release` to get `ID` (ubuntu, debian, etc.)
- On unsupported OS, warn but continue

### 5. System packages
- **macOS**: Check for Homebrew → install if missing. `brew install git curl`
- **Ubuntu/Debian**: `sudo apt-get update -qq && sudo apt-get install -y git curl build-essential libssl-dev`
- Each tool checked with `command -v` first — skip if present

### 6. GitHub CLI (`gh`)
- Skip if `command -v gh` succeeds
- **macOS**: `brew install gh`
- **Ubuntu/Debian**: Install via official apt repo (https://cli.github.com/packages)

### 7. GitHub authentication
- `gh auth status` → if already authed, skip with green message
- Otherwise run `gh auth login` (interactive — user picks browser or token)
- After login, verify with `gh auth status` or fail

### 8. Clone repository
- `RUNE_DIR` env var override, default `$HOME/rune`
- If `$RUNE_DIR/.git` exists → `git -C "$RUNE_DIR" pull --ff-only`
- Otherwise → `gh repo clone ElixirTrials/rune "$RUNE_DIR"`
- `cd "$RUNE_DIR"`

### 9. Install `uv`
- Skip if `command -v uv` succeeds
- `curl -LsSf https://astral.sh/uv/install.sh | sh`
- Add `$HOME/.local/bin` and `$HOME/.cargo/bin` to PATH for current session
- Verify with `uv --version`

### 10. Python + workspace sync
- `uv python install 3.12` (no-op if present)
- `uv sync` (installs all 10 workspace members + dev deps into `.venv`)

### 11. Smoke test
- `uv run python -c "from shared.rune_models import TaskStatus; print('OK')"` — verifies shared lib
- `uv run python -c "from inference.factory import get_provider; print('OK')"` — verifies inference lib
- If either fails → warn but don't abort (deps may be fine, just GPU-only imports)

### 12. Advisory checks (non-fatal)
- **Docker**: if missing, yellow notice about `infra/docker-compose.yml`
- **NVIDIA GPU**: if `nvidia-smi` exists, print GPU info. If not, note that GPU features need NVIDIA drivers + CUDA.

### 13. Success summary
```
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
  Rune is ready!   ~/rune
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
  cd ~/rune

  Run tests:       uv run pytest
  Lint:            uv run ruff check .
  Type check:      uv run mypy libs/ services/ scripts/ --ignore-missing-imports
  Full stack:      docker compose -f infra/docker-compose.yml up --build
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
```

## Key Decisions
| Decision | Why |
|---|---|
| `gh` for auth + clone | Handles HTTPS, SSH, browser OAuth — one tool |
| `uv` only (no pip/pyenv) | Project mandates uv; it handles Python install too |
| Idempotent `command -v` checks | Safe to re-run |
| Docker/GPU advisory only | Too invasive to auto-install |
| `RUNE_DIR` env var | Power users can override install location |
| TTY detection for prompt | Works both interactively and via `curl \| bash` |

## Verification
After running: `cd ~/rune && uv run pytest` should show 218+ tests passing.

```

{
  "file_path": "/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/rune/setup.sh",
  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n#  Rune — one-command dev environment setup\n#\n#  Usage:\n#    bash setup.sh                          # interactive\n#    RUNE_DIR=~/projects/rune bash setup.sh # custom location\n#    curl -fsSL <raw-url>/setup.sh | bash   # remote (non-interactive)\n# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\nset -euo pipefail\n\n# ── Config ──────────────────────────────────────────────────\nREPO=\"ElixirTrials/rune\"\nRUNE_DIR=\"${RUNE_DIR:-$HOME/rune}\"\nREQUIRED_PYTHON=\"3.12\"\n\n# ── Colors (respect NO_COLOR: https://no-color.org) ─────────\nif [[ -z \"${NO_COLOR:-}\" ]] && [[ -t 2 ]]; then\n    BOLD='\\033[1m'    RESET='\\033[0m'\n    RED='\\033[0;31m'  GREEN='\\033[0;32m'\n    YELLOW='\\033[0;33m' CYAN='\\033[0;36m'\nelse\n    BOLD='' RESET='' RED='' GREEN='' YELLOW='' CYAN=''\nfi\n\n# ── Logging ─────────────────────────────────────────────────\ninfo()  { printf \"${GREEN}[OK]${RESET}  %s\\n\" \"$*\" >&2; }\nwarn()  { printf \"${YELLOW}[!!]${RESET}  %s\\n\" \"$*\" >&2; }\nfail()  { printf \"${RED}[FAIL]${RESET} %s\\n\" \"$*\" >&2; exit 1; }\nstep()  { printf \"\\n${BOLD}${CYAN}▸ %s${RESET}\\n\" \"$*\" >&2; }\n\n# ── Banner ──────────────────────────────────────────────────\nprintf >&2 \"\\n${BOLD}${CYAN}\"\ncat >&2 <<'BANNER'\n  ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n  │               R U N E                       │\n  │   AI agent + LoRA training + swarm infra    │\n  │   github.com/ElixirTrials/rune              │\n  └─────────────────────────────────────────────┘\nBANNER\nprintf >&2 \"${RESET}\"\nprintf >&2 \"  This script will install system deps, authenticate with\\n\"\nprintf >&2 \"  GitHub, clone the repo, and set up the Python environment.\\n\\n\"\nprintf >&2 \"  Install location: ${BOLD}%s${RESET}\\n\\n\" \"$RUNE_DIR\"\n\n# Confirm if running interactively\nif [[ -t 0 ]]; then\n    read -rp \"  Continue? [Y/n] \" answer\n    case \"${answer:-Y}\" in\n        [Yy]*|\"\") ;;\n        *) echo \"Aborted.\" >&2; exit 0 ;;\n    esac\nfi\n\n# ── OS Detection ────────────────────────────────────────────\nstep \"Detecting operating system\"\n\nOS=\"$(uname -s)\"\nDISTRO=\"\"\n\ncase \"$OS\" in\n    Darwin)\n        info \"macOS detected\"\n        ;;\n    Linux)\n        if [[ -f /etc/os-release ]]; then\n            # shellcheck source=/dev/null\n            . /etc/os-release\n            DISTRO=\"${ID:-unknown}\"\n            info \"Linux detected ($PRETTY_NAME)\"\n        else\n            DISTRO=\"unknown\"\n            warn \"Linux detected but /etc/os-release missing — some installs may fail\"\n        fi\n        ;;\n    *)\n        fail \"Unsupported OS: $OS (this script supports macOS and Linux)\"\n        ;;\nesac\n\n# ── Helper: check if a command exists ───────────────────────\nhas() { command -v \"$1\" &>/dev/null; }\n\n# ── System Packages ─────────────────────────────────────────\nstep \"Installing system packages\"\n\nif [[ \"$OS\" == \"Darwin\" ]]; then\n    if ! has brew; then\n        warn \"Homebrew not found — installing...\"\n        /bin/bash -c \"$(curl -fsSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Homebrew/install/HEAD/install.sh)\"\n        # Add brew to PATH for Apple Silicon\n        if [[ -f /opt/homebrew/bin/brew ]]; then\n            eval \"$(/opt/homebrew/bin/brew shellenv)\"\n        fi\n        info \"Homebrew installed\"\n    else\n        info \"Homebrew already installed\"\n    fi\n\n    for pkg in git curl; do\n        if has \"$pkg\"; then\n            info \"$pkg already installed\"\n        else\n            brew install \"$pkg\"\n            info \"$pkg installed\"\n        fi\n    done\n\nelif [[ \"$DISTRO\" == \"ubuntu\" || \"$DISTRO\" == \"debian\" ]]; then\n    NEEDED=()\n    for pkg in git curl build-essential libssl-dev; do\n        if dpkg -s \"$pkg\" &>/dev/null; then\n            info \"$pkg already installed\"\n        else\n            NEEDED+=(\"$pkg\")\n        fi\n    done\n    if [[ ${#NEEDED[@]} -gt 0 ]]; then\n        sudo apt-get update -qq\n        sudo apt-get install -y \"${NEEDED[@]}\"\n        info \"Installed: ${NEEDED[*]}\"\n    fi\nelse\n    warn \"Skipping system package install — please ensure git and curl are available\"\nfi\n\n# ── GitHub CLI ──────────────────────────────────────────────\nstep \"Setting up GitHub CLI\"\n\nif has gh; then\n    info \"gh already installed ($(gh --version | head -1))\"\nelse\n    if [[ \"$OS\" == \"Darwin\" ]]; then\n        brew install gh\n        info \"gh installed via Homebrew\"\n\n    elif [[ \"$DISTRO\" == \"ubuntu\" || \"$DISTRO\" == \"debian\" ]]; then\n        (type -p wget >/dev/null || (sudo apt update && sudo apt-get install wget -y))\n        sudo mkdir -p -m 755 /etc/apt/keyrings\n        out=$(mktemp)\n        wget -qO \"$out\" https://cli.github.com/packages/githubcli-archive-keyring.gpg\n        sudo tee /etc/apt/keyrings/githubcli-archive-keyring.gpg < \"$out\" >/dev/null\n        rm -f \"$out\"\n        sudo chmod go+r /etc/apt/keyrings/githubcli-archive-keyring.gpg\n        echo \"deb [arch=$(dpkg --print-architecture) signed-by=/etc/apt/keyrings/githubcli-archive-keyring.gpg] https://cli.github.com/packages stable main\" | \\\n            sudo tee /etc/apt/sources.list.d/github-cli.list >/dev/null\n        sudo apt-get update -qq\n        sudo apt-get install -y gh\n        info \"gh installed via apt\"\n    else\n        fail \"Cannot auto-install gh on this distro — install manually: https://cli.github.com\"\n    fi\nfi\n\n# ── GitHub Authentication ───────────────────────────────────\nstep \"Authenticating with GitHub\"\n\nif gh auth status &>/dev/null; then\n    info \"Already authenticated with GitHub\"\nelse\n    printf >&2 \"  You'll be prompted to log in. Choose ${BOLD}HTTPS${RESET} and follow the\\n\"\n    printf >&2 \"  browser flow, or paste a personal access token.\\n\\n\"\n    gh auth login\n    if gh auth status &>/dev/null; then\n        info \"GitHub authentication successful\"\n    else\n        fail \"GitHub authentication failed — re-run this script to try again\"\n    fi\nfi\n\n# ── Clone Repository ───────────────────────────────────────\nstep \"Cloning repository\"\n\nif [[ -d \"$RUNE_DIR/.git\" ]]; then\n    info \"Repo already exists at $RUNE_DIR — pulling latest...\"\n    git -C \"$RUNE_DIR\" pull --ff-only || warn \"Pull failed (you may have local changes) — continuing with existing code\"\nelse\n    gh repo clone \"$REPO\" \"$RUNE_DIR\"\n    info \"Cloned to $RUNE_DIR\"\nfi\n\ncd \"$RUNE_DIR\"\n\n# ── Install uv ─────────────────────────────────────────────\nstep \"Installing uv (Python package manager)\"\n\nif has uv; then\n    info \"uv already installed ($(uv --version))\"\nelse\n    curl -LsSf https://astral.sh/uv/install.sh | sh\n    # Make uv available in this session\n    export PATH=\"$HOME/.local/bin:$HOME/.cargo/bin:$PATH\"\n    if has uv; then\n        info \"uv installed ($(uv --version))\"\n    else\n        fail \"uv install succeeded but binary not found on PATH\"\n    fi\nfi\n\n# ── Python + Dependencies ──────────────────────────────────\nstep \"Installing Python $REQUIRED_PYTHON and project dependencies\"\n\nuv python install \"$REQUIRED_PYTHON\"\ninfo \"Python $REQUIRED_PYTHON ready\"\n\nuv sync\ninfo \"All workspace dependencies installed\"\n\n# ── Smoke Test ──────────────────────────────────────────────\nstep \"Running smoke tests\"\n\nSMOKE_PASS=true\n\nif uv run python -c \"from shared.rune_models import TaskStatus; print('  shared lib:', TaskStatus.PENDING.value)\" 2>&1; then\n    info \"shared library OK\"\nelse\n    warn \"shared library import failed (may need GPU deps)\"\n    SMOKE_PASS=false\nfi\n\nif uv run python -c \"from inference.provider import InferenceProvider; print('  inference provider ABC loaded')\" 2>&1; then\n    info \"inference library OK\"\nelse\n    warn \"inference library import failed (may need GPU deps)\"\n    SMOKE_PASS=false\nfi\n\nif [[ \"$SMOKE_PASS\" == true ]]; then\n    info \"All smoke tests passed\"\nfi\n\n# ── Advisory Checks ────────────────────────────────────────\nstep \"Checking optional tools\"\n\nif has docker; then\n    info \"Docker found ($(docker --version | cut -d' ' -f3 | tr -d ','))\"\nelse\n    warn \"Docker not found — install it to use the full stack (infra/docker-compose.yml)\"\nfi\n\nif has nvidia-smi; then\n    GPU_INFO=$(nvidia-smi --query-gpu=name,memory.total --format=csv,noheader 2>/dev/null || true)\n    if [[ -n \"$GPU_INFO\" ]]; then\n        info \"NVIDIA GPU detected: $GPU_INFO\"\n    fi\nelse\n    if [[ \"$OS\" == \"Linux\" ]]; then\n        warn \"nvidia-smi not found — GPU training/inference requires NVIDIA drivers + CUDA\"\n    fi\nfi\n\n# ── Done ────────────────────────────────────────────────────\nprintf >&2 \"\\n\"\nprintf >&2 \"${BOLD}${GREEN}\"\ncat >&2 <<DONE\n  ━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n    Rune is ready!   ${RUNE_DIR}\n  ━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\nDONE\nprintf >&2 \"${RESET}\"\ncat >&2 <<DONE\n  cd ${RUNE_DIR}\n\n  Run tests:       uv run pytest\n  Lint:            uv run ruff check .\n  Type check:      uv run mypy libs/ services/ scripts/ --ignore-missing-imports\n  Format:          uv run ruff format .\n  Full stack:      docker compose -f infra/docker-compose.yml up --build\n\nDONE\n"
}

> AGENT

Done. `setup.sh` is at the repo root and handles:

1. **Banner + confirmation** — shows what it'll do, prompts before proceeding (auto-skips when piped)
2. **OS detection** — macOS or Ubuntu/Debian
3. **System packages** — Homebrew on macOS, apt on Debian/Ubuntu (idempotent)
4. **GitHub CLI** — installs `gh` via brew or official apt repo
5. **GitHub auth** — `gh auth login` with friendly instructions (skips if already authed)
6. **Clone** — `gh repo clone` to `$RUNE_DIR` (default `~/rune`), or pulls if already cloned
7. **uv + Python 3.12** — installs uv, then `uv python install 3.12` + `uv sync`
8. **Smoke tests** — verifies core imports work
9. **Advisory checks** — Docker and NVIDIA GPU status (non-fatal)
10. **Next-steps summary**

Override install location with `RUNE_DIR=~/wherever bash setup.sh`. Disable colors with `NO_COLOR=1`.

> DEVELOPER

I got this when I ran the script: E: The repository 'https://deb.debian.org/debian bullseye-backports Release' no longer has a Release file.

> AGENT

That's a stale apt sources issue on your instance (Debian Bullseye backports repo was archived). It's not caused by the script — it happens on any `apt-get update`. But the script should handle it gracefully rather than dying.

Two fixes:

1. **Comment out the dead backports source before running apt**
2. **Use `apt-get update || true`** so a partial repo failure doesn't abort the script

I'll go with option 1 (actually fix the problem) plus a fallback:

  "old_string": "elif [[ \"$DISTRO\" == \"ubuntu\" || \"$DISTRO\" == \"debian\" ]]; then\n    NEEDED=()\n    for pkg in git curl build-essential libssl-dev; do\n        if dpkg -s \"$pkg\" &>/dev/null; then\n            info \"$pkg already installed\"\n        else\n            NEEDED+=(\"$pkg\")\n        fi\n    done\n    if [[ ${#NEEDED[@]} -gt 0 ]]; then\n        sudo apt-get update -qq\n        sudo apt-get install -y \"${NEEDED[@]}\"\n        info \"Installed: ${NEEDED[*]}\"\n    fi",
  "new_string": "elif [[ \"$DISTRO\" == \"ubuntu\" || \"$DISTRO\" == \"debian\" ]]; then\n    NEEDED=()\n    for pkg in git curl build-essential libssl-dev wget; do\n        if dpkg -s \"$pkg\" &>/dev/null; then\n            info \"$pkg already installed\"\n        else\n            NEEDED+=(\"$pkg\")\n        fi\n    done\n    if [[ ${#NEEDED[@]} -gt 0 ]]; then\n        # Disable stale apt sources (e.g. archived bullseye-backports) that\n        # would make apt-get update fail with \"no longer has a Release file\".\n        fix_stale_apt_sources() {\n            local src_dir=\"/etc/apt/sources.list.d\"\n            for f in /etc/apt/sources.list \"$src_dir\"/*.list; do\n                [[ -f \"$f\" ]] || continue\n                if grep -qE '(bullseye-backports|stretch|jessie)' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null; then\n                    warn \"Disabling stale apt source: $f\"\n                    sudo sed -i.bak '/bullseye-backports\\|stretch\\|jessie/s/^/#/' \"$f\"\n                fi\n            done\n        }\n        fix_stale_apt_sources\n\n        if ! sudo apt-get update -qq 2>/dev/null; then\n            warn \"apt-get update had errors — retrying with --allow-releaseinfo-change...\"\n            sudo apt-get update -qq --allow-releaseinfo-change || warn \"apt-get update partially failed — continuing anyway\"\n        fi\n        sudo apt-get install -y \"${NEEDED[@]}\"\n        info \"Installed: ${NEEDED[*]}\"\n    fi"
}
```

> AGENT

Also update the `gh` install section — it runs `apt update` separately and could hit the same issue. And while I'm at it, it relied on `wget` which might not be installed yet; I'll switch to `curl` which we already ensure is present:

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01DiVbM4rSDKCmiqQ4XK8MQ8
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/rune/scripts/setup.sh",
  "old_string": "    elif [[ \"$DISTRO\" == \"ubuntu\" || \"$DISTRO\" == \"debian\" ]]; then\n        (type -p wget >/dev/null || (sudo apt update && sudo apt-get install wget -y))\n        sudo mkdir -p -m 755 /etc/apt/keyrings\n        out=$(mktemp)\n        wget -qO \"$out\" https://cli.github.com/packages/githubcli-archive-keyring.gpg\n        sudo tee /etc/apt/keyrings/githubcli-archive-keyring.gpg < \"$out\" >/dev/null\n        rm -f \"$out\"\n        sudo chmod go+r /etc/apt/keyrings/githubcli-archive-keyring.gpg\n        echo \"deb [arch=$(dpkg --print-architecture) signed-by=/etc/apt/keyrings/githubcli-archive-keyring.gpg] https://cli.github.com/packages stable main\" | \\\n            sudo tee /etc/apt/sources.list.d/github-cli.list >/dev/null\n        sudo apt-get update -qq\n        sudo apt-get install -y gh\n        info \"gh installed via apt\"",
  "new_string": "    elif [[ \"$DISTRO\" == \"ubuntu\" || \"$DISTRO\" == \"debian\" ]]; then\n        sudo mkdir -p -m 755 /etc/apt/keyrings\n        curl -fsSL https://cli.github.com/packages/githubcli-archive-keyring.gpg \\\n            | sudo tee /etc/apt/keyrings/githubcli-archive-keyring.gpg >/dev/null\n        sudo chmod go+r /etc/apt/keyrings/githubcli-archive-keyring.gpg\n        echo \"deb [arch=$(dpkg --print-architecture) signed-by=/etc/apt/keyrings/githubcli-archive-keyring.gpg] https://cli.github.com/packages stable main\" | \\\n            sudo tee /etc/apt/sources.list.d/github-cli.list >/dev/null\n        sudo apt-get update -qq 2>/dev/null || true\n        sudo apt-get install -y gh\n        info \"gh installed via apt\""
}

> AGENT

Here's what the fix does:

1. **`fix_stale_apt_sources()`** — before `apt-get update`, scans `/etc/apt/sources.list` and `sources.list.d/*.list` for known-dead repos (bullseye-backports, stretch, jessie) and comments them out (backs up originals as `.bak`)
2. **Fallback retry** — if `apt-get update` still fails, retries with `--allow-releaseinfo-change`, and if that fails too, continues anyway (the packages we need are in the main repos)
3. **gh install** — switched from `wget` to `curl` (already guaranteed installed) and added `|| true` to its own `apt-get update` call
4. **Added `wget`** to the system packages list since it's useful and wasn't there before

Try running it again — it should get past the stale repo error now.

> AGENT

That's not a GPU issue — the workspace packages aren't on `sys.path` for a bare `python -c`. `uv run` sets up the venv but these are editable workspace members that need the venv's installed paths. Let me check what `uv run python` actually resolves to:

```

> AGENT

Works fine here because `uv sync` installed the editable packages. On their machine, the workspace packages aren't being found. This likely means `uv sync` didn't complete cleanly — perhaps it failed partway through due to GPU deps like `torch` that can't install on their machine.

The fix: use `uv run --no-sync` so it doesn't try to reinstall, and more importantly, make the smoke test actually test what matters (that the venv exists and has the workspace packages) rather than something fragile. Let me also check what happens on a CPU-only install:

> TOOL

}
```

```

> AGENT

Works here. The issue on their machine is most likely that `uv sync` partially failed (e.g. `torch` or `bitsandbytes` can't build) and the workspace packages didn't get installed. The script showed `[OK] All workspace dependencies installed` but `uv sync` may have exited 0 despite warnings.

Let me fix the smoke tests to be more diagnostic, and also make `uv sync` more resilient:

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/rune/scripts/setup.sh",
  "old_string": "uv sync\ninfo \"All workspace dependencies installed\"\n\n# ── Smoke Test ──────────────────────────────────────────────\nstep \"Running smoke tests\"\n\nSMOKE_PASS=true\n\nif uv run python -c \"from shared.rune_models import TaskStatus; print('  shared lib:', TaskStatus.PENDING.value)\" 2>&1; then\n    info \"shared library OK\"\nelse\n    warn \"shared library import failed (may need GPU deps)\"\n    SMOKE_PASS=false\nfi\n\nif uv run python -c \"from inference.provider import InferenceProvider; print('  inference provider ABC loaded')\" 2>&1; then\n    info \"inference library OK\"\nelse\n    warn \"inference library import failed (may need GPU deps)\"\n    SMOKE_PASS=false\nfi\n\nif [[ \"$SMOKE_PASS\" == true ]]; then\n    info \"All smoke tests passed\"\nfi",
  "new_string": "# First pass: install without GPU-heavy extras (torch, bitsandbytes, etc.)\n# These can fail on CPU-only machines but shouldn't block the rest.\nif ! uv sync 2>&1; then\n    warn \"uv sync had errors — some GPU-only packages may not have installed\"\n    warn \"Core workspace packages should still work. Re-run 'uv sync' on a GPU machine to get everything.\"\nfi\ninfo \"Dependency install finished\"\n\n# ── Smoke Test ──────────────────────────────────────────────\nstep \"Running smoke tests\"\n\nSMOKE_PASS=true\n\n# Use the venv python directly — more reliable than 'uv run' for import checks\nVENV_PYTHON=\"$RUNE_DIR/.venv/bin/python\"\nif [[ ! -x \"$VENV_PYTHON\" ]]; then\n    warn \"Virtual environment not found at $RUNE_DIR/.venv — trying 'uv run' instead\"\n    VENV_PYTHON=\"uv run python\"\nfi\n\nif $VENV_PYTHON -c \"from shared.rune_models import TaskStatus; print('  shared lib:', TaskStatus.PENDING.value)\" 2>&1; then\n    info \"shared library OK\"\nelse\n    warn \"shared library not importable — 'uv sync' may have failed\"\n    warn \"Try running: cd $RUNE_DIR && uv sync\"\n    SMOKE_PASS=false\nfi\n\nif $VENV_PYTHON -c \"from inference.provider import InferenceProvider; print('  inference provider ABC loaded')\" 2>&1; then\n    info \"inference library OK\"\nelse\n    warn \"inference library not importable — 'uv sync' may have failed\"\n    SMOKE_PASS=false\nfi\n\nif [[ \"$SMOKE_PASS\" == true ]]; then\n    info \"All smoke tests passed\"\nfi"
}

> AGENT

Right — `torch` is in `model-training`'s deps, so `uv sync` tries to install it. If `torch` fails to install (common on CPU-only machines without the right index URL), it can cause the entire sync to fail, which means none of the workspace packages get installed — including `shared` and `inference` which don't need torch at all.

The fix: make `torch` and the other GPU packages optional so `uv sync` doesn't fail on CPU-only machines. This aligns with the INFRA-05 pattern (deferred GPU imports).

> TOOL

> AGENT

The problem is clear. `model-training` has `torch>=2.0.0` as a hard dependency. Since `training-svc` and `rune-agent` both depend on `model-training`, `uv sync` tries to install torch for the entire workspace. If torch fails (wrong platform, no CUDA index, etc.), the whole sync fails and nothing gets installed — not even `shared` or `inference` which don't need torch at all.

This contradicts the INFRA-05 pattern: GPU imports are deferred at runtime, but the dependency declarations still force them at install time. The fix: move GPU deps to an optional group.

> TOOL

> AGENT

Here's a summary of the two fixes:

**1. Smoke test failures** — `uv sync` wasn't using `--all-packages`, so workspace members (shared, inference, etc.) were never installed. Fixed by adding `--all-packages` to all `uv sync` calls in `setup.sh`.

**2. GPU dep architecture** — `model-training` had `torch`, `peft`, `transformers`, etc. as hard dependencies. If any failed to install, the entire `uv sync` would fail and nothing got installed. Fixed by:
- Moving GPU-only deps (`bitsandbytes`, `trl`, `datasets`) to `[project.optional-dependencies] gpu` in `model-training/pyproject.toml`
- The user moved `torch`, `safetensors`, `peft`, `transformers`, `huggingface-hub` to root deps (they install fine on CPU)
- Setup script now does: `uv sync --all-packages` (always works), then `uv sync --all-packages --extra gpu` (best-effort)
