   168→This script must:
   169→1. Accept CLI args (use argparse, no new deps):
   170→     --base-model       str   default=RUNE_BASE_MODEL env
   171→     --checkpoint       str   default=RUNE_HYPERNETWORK_CHECKPOINT env
   172→     --output-dir       str   required
   173→     --data-path        str   required  (JSONL file: {doc, queries, answers})
   174→     --rank             int   default=8
   175→     --lora-target      str   choices=["mlp.down_proj","q_proj_v_proj"] default="mlp.down_proj"
   176→     --epochs           int   default=3
   177→     --lr               float default=1e-4
   178→     --batch-size       int   default=2
   179→     --grad-accum       int   default=8
   180→     --max-doc-len      int   default=2048
   181→     --chunk-size       int   default=512
   182→     --hidden-layer     int   default=-1
   183→     --bf16             flag  default=True
   184→     --flash-attn       flag  default=True
   185→     --dry-run          flag  (shape-check + 1 batch, then exit)
   186→     --smoke-test       flag  (5 steps, tiny data subset)
   187→
   188→2. Training loop:
   189→   a. Load frozen Qwen3-Coder-Next with device_map="auto", torch_dtype=bfloat16.
   190→      Freeze all parameters (requires_grad=False).
   191→   b. Load hypernetwork from checkpoint (partial transfer per strategy above).
   192→      Only retrained components have requires_grad=True.
   193→   c. Data loading: read JSONL where each line is
   194→        {"doc": "...", "queries": ["q1","q2",...], "answers": ["a1","a2",...]}
   195→      For each (doc, query, answer) triple, this is one training example.
   196→   d. Teacher forward pass: Qwen3-Coder-Next with doc+query in context →
   197→      extract logits and hidden states (for distillation target and
   198→      modality projection input respectively).
   199→   e. Hypernetwork forward: extract_hidden_states(doc) → modality_proj →
   200→      Perceiver encoder → weight_head → LoRA adapter weights.
   201→   f. Student forward pass: inject generated LoRA adapter into Qwen3-Coder-Next
   202→      using a temporary PEFT adapter load (or manual weight injection) →
   203→      run with query only (no doc) → get student logits.
   204→   g. Loss: KL divergence between teacher logits and student logits,
   205→      computed over the answer token positions only.
   206→      Also log: teacher_loss (NLL on answer), student_loss (NLL on answer),
   207→      kl_loss, adapter_gen_latency_ms, gpu_mem_gb.
   208→   h. Backprop through hypernetwork only (frozen parts have no grad).
   209→   i. AdamW optimizer, cosine LR schedule with warmup (warmup_ratio=0.05).
   210→   j. Save checkpoint every 500 steps and at end:
   211→        torch.save({
   212→            "model_state_dict": hypernetwork.state_dict(),
   213→            "hypernetwork_config": <ctor kwargs dict>,
   214→            "step": step,
   215→            "base_model_id": BASE_MODEL_ID,
   216→        }, output_dir/checkpoint_{step}.pt)
   217→
   218→3. --dry-run mode:
   219→   - Load models, build one batch, run one forward pass.
   220→   - Print all tensor shapes: hidden_states, modality_proj output,
   221→     latents before/after cross-attend, weight_head output,
   222→     adapter shapes per layer, student logits, KL loss value.
   223→   - Assert all shapes match expectations.
   224→   - Exit 0 on success.
   225→
   226→4. Student LoRA injection:
   227→   Implement a clean helper:
   228→     def inject_lora_adapter(
   229→         base_model,
   230→         adapter_state_dict: dict[str, torch.Tensor],
   231→         lora_target: str,
   232→         rank: int,
   233→         alpha: int,
   234→     ) -> context_manager:
   235→         ...
   236→   It must use PEFT's get_peft_model with a LoraConfig matching
   237→   the generated adapter, load the state_dict, then yield the model,
   238→   and on exit restore the base model to its original state
   239→   (no side effects between training steps).

    32→     down_proj: out_features = cfg.hidden_size

    35→     q_proj: out_features = cfg.hidden_size
    36→     k_proj: out_features = head_dim * cfg.num_key_value_heads

    45→The hypernetwork has two conceptual halves:
    46→
    47→FROZEN (copy exactly from checkpoint, requires_grad=False):
    48→  • Perceiver encoder core
    49→      - 8 cross-attention blocks (cross_attend in current code)
    50→      - 512-dim internal dimension
    51→      - Self-attention stack (self_attend)
    52→      - Learnable latents tensor (latents parameter)
    53→    Rationale: Perceiver learned a general "document → latent
    54→    representation" mapping that is architecture-agnostic.
    55→
    56→RETRAINED (do not copy weights, requires_grad=True):
    57→  • Modality projection layer
    58→      - Maps Qwen3-Coder-Next hidden_size → 512-dim Perceiver space
    59→      - This replaces the token_embedding in the current code.
    60→        The new design must extract per-layer hidden states from the
    61→        frozen Qwen3-Coder-Next backbone (see "Architecture change"
    62→        below) and project them into Perceiver input space.
    63→      - Initialize randomly (Kaiming uniform).
    64→  • Decoder latent queries
    65→      - The weight_head linear layer (num_latents*latent_dim → total_params)
    66→        has changed output shape because Qwen3-Coder-Next has a different
    67→        num_layers and different down_proj dims.
    68→      - Initialize the new weight_head by interpolating the old
    69→        weight_head's output rows: use torch.nn.functional.interpolate
    70→        or linear interpolation along the output dimension to map
    71→        old total_params → new total_params as a warm initialization.
    72→      - If the rank dimension is unchanged (rank=8), the A/B matrix
    73→        structure is preserved and you can interpolate layer-by-layer.
    74→  • Weight generation head output dims
    75→      - Must match Qwen3-Coder-Next's down_proj shape exactly.
    76→      - Validate with an assert at construction time:
    77→          expected = num_layers * (rank * intermediate_size + hidden_size * rank)
    78→          assert weight_head.out_features == expected
    79→  • All bias parameters — reinitialize to zero.

   175→     --lora-target      str   choices=["mlp.down_proj","q_proj_v_proj"] default="mlp.down_proj"
   176→     --epochs           int   default=3
   177→     --lr               float default=1e-4
   178→     --batch-size       int   default=2
   179→     --grad-accum       int   default=8
   180→     --max-doc-len      int   default=2048
   181→     --chunk-size       int   default=512
   182→     --hidden-layer     int   default=-1
   183→     --bf16             flag  default=True
   184→     --flash-attn       flag  default=True
   185→     --dry-run          flag  (shape-check + 1 batch, then exit)
   186→     --smoke-test       flag  (5 steps, tiny data subset)
   187→
   188→2. Training loop:
   189→   a. Load frozen Qwen3-Coder-Next with device_map="auto", torch_dtype=bfloat16.
   190→      Freeze all parameters (requires_grad=False).
   191→   b. Load hypernetwork from checkpoint (partial transfer per strategy above).
   192→      Only retrained components have requires_grad=True.
   193→   c. Data loading: read JSONL where each line is
   194→        {"doc": "...", "queries": ["q1","q2",...], "answers": ["a1","a2",...]}
   195→      For each (doc, query, answer) triple, this is one training example.
   196→   d. Teacher forward pass: Qwen3-Coder-Next with doc+query in context →
   197→      extract logits and hidden states (for distillation target and
   198→      modality projection input respectively).
   199→   e. Hypernetwork forward: extract_hidden_states(doc) → modality_proj →
   200→      Perceiver encoder → weight_head → LoRA adapter weights.
   201→   f. Student forward pass: inject generated LoRA adapter into Qwen3-Coder-Next
   202→      using a temporary PEFT adapter load (or manual weight injection) →
   203→      run with query only (no doc) → get student logits.
   204→   g. Loss: KL divergence between teacher logits and student logits,
   205→      computed over the answer token positions only.
   206→      Also log: teacher_loss (NLL on answer), student_loss (NLL on answer),
   207→      kl_loss, adapter_gen_latency_ms, gpu_mem_gb.
   208→   h. Backprop through hypernetwork only (frozen parts have no grad).
   209→   i. AdamW optimizer, cosine LR schedule with warmup (warmup_ratio=0.05).
   210→   j. Save checkpoint every 500 steps and at end:
   211→        torch.save({
   212→            "model_state_dict": hypernetwork.state_dict(),
   213→            "hypernetwork_config": <ctor kwargs dict>,
   214→            "step": step,
   215→            "base_model_id": BASE_MODEL_ID,
   216→        }, output_dir/checkpoint_{step}.pt)
   217→
   218→3. --dry-run mode:
   219→   - Load models, build one batch, run one forward pass.
   220→   - Print all tensor shapes: hidden_states, modality_proj output,
   221→     latents before/after cross-attend, weight_head output,
   222→     adapter shapes per layer, student logits, KL loss value.
   223→   - Assert all shapes match expectations.
   224→   - Exit 0 on success.
   225→
   226→4. Student LoRA injection:
   227→   Implement a clean helper:
   228→     def inject_lora_adapter(
   229→         base_model,
   230→         adapter_state_dict: dict[str, torch.Tensor],
   231→         lora_target: str,
   232→         rank: int,
   233→         alpha: int,
   234→     ) -> context_manager:
   235→         ...
   236→   It must use PEFT's get_peft_model with a LoraConfig matching
   237→   the generated adapter, load the state_dict, then yield the model,
   238→   and on exit restore the base model to its original state
   239→   (no side effects between training steps).
   240→   This avoids repeated model reload overhead.
   241→

     1→"""SakanaAI Doc-to-LoRA integration.
     2→
     3→Wraps Sakana's pretrained HyperLoRA perceiver so it can be used through
     4→our hypernetwork interface (load_pretrained → generate_adapter).
     5→
     6→The Sakana hypernetwork takes per-layer activations from a base model as
     7→input and produces LoRA adapter weights.  This module handles:
     8→  - Downloading the checkpoint from HuggingFace
     9→  - Patching flash-attention assertions for CPU/MPS/non-flash environments
    10→  - Extracting per-layer activations from the base model
    11→  - Saving the generated LoRA weights in PEFT format
    12→
    13→GPU imports are deferred inside function bodies per INFRA-05 pattern.
    14→"""
    15→
    16→from __future__ import annotations
    17→
    18→import json
    19→import logging
    20→from pathlib import Path
    21→from typing import Any
    22→
    23→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    24→
    25→# HuggingFace repo for Sakana's pretrained checkpoints
    26→HF_REPO_ID = "SakanaAI/doc-to-lora"
    27→# Available checkpoints: gemma_2b_d2l, gemma_demo, mistral_7b_d2l, qwen_4b_d2l
    28→DEFAULT_VARIANT = "gemma_demo"
    29→DEFAULT_HF_FILENAME = f"{DEFAULT_VARIANT}/checkpoint-80000/pytorch_model.bin"
    30→LOCAL_CACHE_DIR = Path.home() / ".cache" / "rune" / "sakana_d2l"
    31→
    32→
    33→def _patch_flash_attention() -> None:
    34→    """Patch Sakana's idefics2 module to work without flash_attn.
    35→
    36→    Replaces flash attention classes and assertions with eager equivalents
    37→    so the perceiver can run on CPU/MPS/CUDA without flash_attn installed.
    38→    """
    39→    import torch  # noqa: PLC0415
    40→    import torch.nn as nn  # noqa: PLC0415
    41→
    42→    import ctx_to_lora.modeling.idefics2 as idefics2_mod  # noqa: PLC0415
    43→    from ctx_to_lora.modeling.idefics2 import (  # noqa: PLC0415
    44→        Idefics2Perceiver,
    45→        Idefics2PerceiverAttention,
    46→        Idefics2PerceiverConfig,
    47→        Idefics2PerceiverResampler,
    48→    )
    49→
    50→    # Map both eager and flash_attention_2 to the eager attention class
    51→    idefics2_mod.IDEFICS2_PERCEIVER_ATTENTION_CLASSES["eager"] = (
    52→        Idefics2PerceiverAttention
    53→    )
    54→    idefics2_mod.IDEFICS2_PERCEIVER_ATTENTION_CLASSES["flash_attention_2"] = (
    55→        Idefics2PerceiverAttention
    56→    )
    57→
    58→    # Patch eager attention forward to accept flash-only kwargs
    59→    _orig_attn_fwd = Idefics2PerceiverAttention.forward
    60→
    61→    def _patched_attn_fwd(
    62→        self: Any, *args: Any, is_cross_attn: Any = None, **kwargs: Any
    63→    ) -> Any:
    64→        kwargs.pop("cu_seq_lens_q", None)
    65→        kwargs.pop("cu_seq_lens_k", None)
    66→        kwargs.pop("max_length_q", None)
    67→        kwargs.pop("max_length_k", None)
    68→        return _orig_attn_fwd(self, *args, **kwargs)
    69→
    70→    Idefics2PerceiverAttention.forward = _patched_attn_fwd
    71→
    72→    # Patch resampler forward to use eager path
    73→    def _eager_resampler_forward(
    74→        self: Any,
    75→        context: torch.Tensor,
    76→        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    77→        position_ids: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    78→    ) -> torch.Tensor:
    79→        if position_ids is None:
    80→            bsz = context.shape[0]
    81→        else:
    82→            bsz = torch.where(position_ids == 0, 1, 0).sum().item()
    83→        latents = self.latents_q.unsqueeze(0).expand((bsz, *self.latents_q.size()))
    84→        compressed_context = latents
    85→        for layer in self.layers:
    86→            layer_outputs = layer(
    87→                latents=compressed_context,
    88→                context=context,
    89→                attention_mask=None,
    90→                position_ids=None,
    91→                past_key_value=None,
    92→                output_attentions=False,
    93→                use_cache=False,
    94→            )
    95→            compressed_context = layer_outputs[0]
    96→        return self.layernorm(compressed_context)
    97→
    98→    Idefics2PerceiverResampler.forward = _eager_resampler_forward
    99→
   100→    # Patch resampler __init__ to bypass flash_attention_2 assertion
   101→    _orig_resampler_init = Idefics2PerceiverResampler.__init__
   102→
   103→    def _patched_resampler_init(self: Any, config: Any) -> None:
   104→        config._attn_implementation = "flash_attention_2"
   105→        _orig_resampler_init(self, config)
   106→        self._use_flash_attention_2 = False
   107→
   108→    Idefics2PerceiverResampler.__init__ = _patched_resampler_init
   109→
   110→    # Patch Perceiver init to force eager on sub-configs
   111→    _orig_perceiver_init = Idefics2Perceiver.__init__
   112→
   113→    def _patched_perceiver_init(self: Any, enc_cfg: Any, dec_cfg: Any) -> None:
   114→        enc_cfg._attn_implementation = "eager"
   115→        enc_cfg._attn_implementation_internal = "eager"
   116→        dec_cfg._attn_implementation = "eager"
   117→        dec_cfg._attn_implementation_internal = "eager"
   118→        _orig_perceiver_init(self, enc_cfg, dec_cfg)
   119→
   120→    Idefics2Perceiver.__init__ = _patched_perceiver_init
   121→
   122→    # Patch PerceiverConfig init to default to eager
   123→    _orig_cfg_init = Idefics2PerceiverConfig.__init__
   124→
   125→    def _patched_cfg_init(self: Any, *args: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> None:
   126→        kwargs["attn_implementation"] = "eager"
   127→        _orig_cfg_init(self, *args, **kwargs)
   128→        self._attn_implementation = "eager"
   129→        self._attn_implementation_internal = "eager"
   130→
   131→    Idefics2PerceiverConfig.__init__ = _patched_cfg_init
   132→
   133→
   134→def download_checkpoint(
   135→    variant: str = DEFAULT_VARIANT,
   136→) -> Path:
   137→    """Download Sakana's pretrained checkpoint from HuggingFace.
   138→
   139→    Args:
   140→        variant: Which checkpoint variant to download.
   141→            Options: 'gemma_demo', 'gemma_2b_d2l', 'mistral_7b_d2l', 'qwen_4b_d2l'.
   142→
   143→    Returns:
   144→        Path to the downloaded checkpoint file.
   145→    """
   146→    # Determine filename based on variant
   147→    if variant == "gemma_demo":
   148→        hf_filename = "gemma_demo/checkpoint-80000/pytorch_model.bin"
   149→    elif variant in ("gemma_2b_d2l", "mistral_7b_d2l", "qwen_4b_d2l"):
   150→        hf_filename = f"{variant}/checkpoint-20000/pytorch_model.bin"
   151→    else:
   152→        msg = f"Unknown variant: {variant}"
   153→        raise ValueError(msg)
   154→
   155→    cached = LOCAL_CACHE_DIR / variant / "pytorch_model.bin"
   156→    if cached.exists():
   157→        logger.info("Using cached Sakana checkpoint: %s", cached)
   158→        return cached
   159→
   160→    from huggingface_hub import hf_hub_download  # noqa: PLC0415
   161→
   162→    logger.info("Downloading Sakana checkpoint %s from %s...", variant, HF_REPO_ID)
   163→    downloaded = Path(hf_hub_download(repo_id=HF_REPO_ID, filename=hf_filename))
   164→
   165→    cached.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   166→    import shutil  # noqa: PLC0415
   167→
   168→    shutil.copy2(downloaded, cached)
   169→    logger.info("Cached to: %s", cached)
   170→    return cached
   171→
   172→
   173→def load_sakana_checkpoint(
   174→    checkpoint_path: str | Path | None = None,
   175→    variant: str = DEFAULT_VARIANT,
   176→    device: str = "cpu",
   177→) -> tuple[Any, Any]:
   178→    """Load Sakana's HyperLoRA perceiver from checkpoint.
   179→
   180→    Downloads from HuggingFace if no local path is provided.
   181→    Patches flash attention for CPU/MPS compatibility.
   182→
   183→    Args:
   184→        checkpoint_path: Path to local checkpoint. If None, downloads from HF.
   185→        variant: HF checkpoint variant (only used if checkpoint_path is None).
   186→        device: Device to load onto.
   187→
   188→    Returns:
   189→        Tuple of (hypernet, hypernet_config).
   190→    """
   191→    import torch  # noqa: PLC0415
   192→
   193→    _patch_flash_attention()
   194→
   195→    if checkpoint_path is None:
   196→        checkpoint_path = download_checkpoint(variant)
   197→
   198→    logger.info("Loading Sakana checkpoint: %s", checkpoint_path)
   199→    sd = torch.load(str(checkpoint_path), map_location="cpu", weights_only=False)
   200→
   201→    hc = sd["hypernet_config"]
   202→    logger.info(
   203→        "HypernetConfig: latent_size=%d, lora_r=%d, base_model=%s",
   204→        hc.latent_size,
   205→        hc.lora_config.r,
   206→        sd["base_model_name_or_path"],
   207→    )
   208→
   209→    from ctx_to_lora.modeling.hypernet import HyperLoRA  # noqa: PLC0415
   210→
   211→    hypernet = HyperLoRA(hc).to(torch.float32)
   212→
   213→    # Load perceiver/aggregator/head weights from checkpoint
   214→    hypernet_keys = [
   215→        k for k in sd.keys() if k.startswith(("aggregator.", "head.", "extra_"))
   216→    ]
   217→    hypernet_sd = {k: sd[k] for k in hypernet_keys if k in hypernet.state_dict()}
   218→
   219→    if hypernet_sd:
   220→        loaded = hypernet.load_state_dict(hypernet_sd, strict=False)
   221→        logger.info("Loaded %d perceiver weight tensors", len(hypernet_sd))
   222→        if loaded.missing_keys:
   223→            logger.info("Missing keys: %d", len(loaded.missing_keys))
   224→    else:
   225→        full_state = {k: v for k, v in sd.items() if isinstance(v, torch.Tensor)}
   226→        loaded = hypernet.load_state_dict(full_state, strict=False)
   227→        logger.info(
   228→            "Loaded full state: missing=%d, unexpected=%d",
   229→            len(loaded.missing_keys),
   230→            len(loaded.unexpected_keys),
   231→        )
   232→
   233→    hypernet = hypernet.to(device)
   234→    hypernet.eval()
   235→
   236→    param_count = sum(p.numel() for p in hypernet.parameters())
   237→    logger.info("HyperLoRA params: %d", param_count)
   238→
   239→    return hypernet, hc
   240→
   241→
   242→def extract_activations(
   243→    text: str,
   244→    base_model_name: str,
   245→    layer_indices: list[int],
   246→    device: str = "cpu",
   247→    max_length: int = 512,
   248→) -> tuple[Any, Any]:
   249→    """Extract per-layer hidden state activations from the base model.
   250→
   251→    Runs the input text through the base model and captures hidden states
   252→    at the specified layer indices. These activations are what Sakana's
   253→    HyperLoRA perceiver expects as input.
   254→
   255→    Args:
   256→        text: Input text to process.
   257→        base_model_name: HuggingFace model ID for the base model.
   258→        layer_indices: Which layers to extract activations from.
   259→        device: Device for computation.
   260→        max_length: Max token sequence length.
   261→
   262→    Returns:
   263→        Tuple of (features, attention_mask) ready for HyperLoRA.
   264→        features shape: (1, num_layers, seq_len, hidden_dim)
   265→        attention_mask shape: (1, seq_len)
   266→    """
   267→    import torch  # noqa: PLC0415
   268→    from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer  # noqa: PLC0415
   269→
   270→    logger.info("Loading base model %s for activation extraction...", base_model_name)
   271→    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(base_model_name)
   272→    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(
   273→        base_model_name,
   274→        output_hidden_states=True,
   275→        torch_dtype=torch.float32,
   276→    )
   277→    model.to(device)
   278→    model.eval()
   279→
   280→    inputs = tokenizer(
   281→        text, return_tensors="pt", truncation=True, max_length=max_length
   282→    )
   283→    inputs = {k: v.to(device) for k, v in inputs.items()}
   284→
   285→    with torch.no_grad():
   286→        outputs = model(**inputs)
   287→
   288→    # outputs.hidden_states is a tuple of (num_layers+1,) tensors
   289→    # each of shape (batch, seq_len, hidden_dim)
   290→    hidden_states = outputs.hidden_states
   291→
   292→    # Stack selected layers: (batch, num_layers, seq_len, hidden_dim)
   293→    selected = torch.stack([hidden_states[i] for i in layer_indices], dim=1)
   294→    attention_mask = inputs["attention_mask"]
   295→
   296→    logger.info(
   297→        "Extracted activations: %s from %d layers", selected.shape, len(layer_indices)
   298→    )
   299→
   300→    # Free base model memory
   301→    del model
   302→    if device != "cpu":
   303→        torch.cuda.empty_cache() if "cuda" in device else None
   304→
   305→    return selected, attention_mask
   306→
   307→
   308→def generate_adapter_from_sakana(
   309→    text: str,
   310→    output_dir: str,
   311→    checkpoint_path: str | Path | None = None,
   312→    variant: str = DEFAULT_VARIANT,
   313→    base_model_name: str | None = None,
   314→    device: str = "cpu",
   315→) -> str:
   316→    """End-to-end: text → base model activations → HyperLoRA → PEFT adapter.
   317→
   318→    This is the main entry point. It:
   319→      1. Loads the Sakana pretrained perceiver (downloading if needed)
   320→      2. Runs text through the base model to get per-layer activations
   321→      3. Feeds activations through the perceiver to generate LoRA weights
   322→      4. Saves weights in PEFT-compatible format
   323→
   324→    Args:
   325→        text: Input text (trajectory, document, context) to encode.
   326→        output_dir: Directory to save the PEFT adapter files.
   327→        checkpoint_path: Path to local checkpoint, or None to download.
   328→        variant: HF checkpoint variant if downloading.
   329→        base_model_name: Override base model. If None, uses the one from checkpoint.
   330→        device: Device for computation.
   331→
   332→    Returns:
   333→        Path to the saved adapter directory.
   334→    """
   335→    import torch  # noqa: PLC0415
   336→
   337→    hypernet, hc = load_sakana_checkpoint(checkpoint_path, variant, device)
   338→
   339→    # Determine base model from checkpoint config
   340→    if base_model_name is None:
   341→        # Load checkpoint just to read base_model_name_or_path
   342→        if checkpoint_path is None:
   343→            checkpoint_path = download_checkpoint(variant)
   344→        sd = torch.load(str(checkpoint_path), map_location="cpu", weights_only=False)
   345→        base_model_name = sd["base_model_name_or_path"]
   346→        del sd
   347→
   348→    logger.info("Base model: %s", base_model_name)
   349→
   350→    # Extract activations from base model
   351→    layer_indices = list(hc.layer_indices)
   352→    features, attn_mask = extract_activations(
   353→        text=text,
   354→        base_model_name=base_model_name,
   355→        layer_indices=layer_indices,
   356→        device=device,
   357→        max_length=512,
   358→    )
   359→
   360→    # Generate LoRA weights via perceiver
   361→    logger.info("Generating LoRA weights via HyperLoRA perceiver...")
   362→    with torch.no_grad():
   363→        lora_dict, layernorm_dict = hypernet.generate_weights(
   364→            features, attn_mask, None
   365→        )
   366→
   367→    # Save as PEFT adapter
   368→    _save_sakana_adapter(
   369→        lora_dict=lora_dict,
   370→        output_dir=output_dir,
   371→        base_model_name=base_model_name,
   372→        hc=hc,
   373→    )
   374→
   375→    return output_dir
   376→
   377→
   378→def _save_sakana_adapter(
   379→    lora_dict: dict[str, dict[str, Any]],
   380→    output_dir: str,
   381→    base_model_name: str,
   382→    hc: Any,
   383→) -> None:
   384→    """Save Sakana's HyperLoRA output as a PEFT-compatible adapter.
   385→
   386→    Converts from Sakana's format (dict of module → {A, B} with batch/layer dims)
   387→    to PEFT's flat state_dict format.
   388→
   389→    Args:
   390→        lora_dict: Output from HyperLoRA.generate_weights().
   391→        output_dir: Directory to write adapter files.
   392→        base_model_name: Base model identifier for adapter config.
   393→        hc: HypernetConfig with lora rank and target modules.
   394→    """
   395→    from safetensors.torch import save_file  # noqa: PLC0415
   396→
   397→    output_path = Path(output_dir)
   398→    output_path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   399→
   400→    layer_indices = list(hc.layer_indices)
   401→    target_modules = list(hc.lora_config.target_modules)
   402→    rank = hc.lora_config.r
   403→
   404→    # Convert Sakana format → PEFT flat state_dict
   405→    # Sakana: lora_dict[module_name]["A"] shape (batch, num_layers, rank, in_features)
   406→    # PEFT: base_model.model.model.layers.{i}.mlp.{module}.lora_A.weight (rank, in_features)
   407→    state_dict = {}
   408→    for mod_name, weights in lora_dict.items():
   409→        if mod_name not in target_modules:
   410→            continue
   411→        a_weights = weights["A"]  # (batch, num_layers, rank, in_features)
   412→        b_weights = weights["B"]  # (batch, num_layers, rank, out_features)
   413→
   414→        for layer_pos, layer_idx in enumerate(layer_indices):
   415→            key_a = (
   416→                f"base_model.model.model.layers.{layer_idx}"
   417→                f".mlp.{mod_name}.lora_A.weight"
   418→            )
   419→            key_b = (
   420→                f"base_model.model.model.layers.{layer_idx}"
   421→                f".mlp.{mod_name}.lora_B.weight"
   422→            )
   423→            # Take first batch element, transpose B to (out_features, rank)
   424→            state_dict[key_a] = a_weights[0, layer_pos].contiguous()
   425→            state_dict[key_b] = b_weights[0, layer_pos].t().contiguous()
   426→
   427→    save_file(state_dict, str(output_path / "adapter_model.safetensors"))
   428→
   429→    adapter_config = {
   430→        "peft_type": "LORA",
   431→        "r": rank,
   432→        "lora_alpha": rank * 2,
   433→        "target_modules": target_modules,
   434→        "lora_dropout": 0.0,
   435→        "bias": "none",
   436→        "task_type": "CAUSAL_LM",
   437→        "base_model_name_or_path": base_model_name,
   438→        "inference_mode": True,
   439→        "modules_to_save": None,
   440→        "fan_in_fan_out": False,
   441→    }
   442→    (output_path / "adapter_config.json").write_text(json.dumps(adapter_config, indent=2))
   443→
   444→    logger.info(
   445→        "Saved PEFT adapter: %d tensors, %d layers, rank=%d, targets=%s",
   446→        len(state_dict),
   447→        len(layer_indices),
   448→        rank,
   449→        target_modules,
   450→    )
   451→
```

No modules_to_save in LoraConfig/adapter_config.json — vLLM rejects\n   these (see save_hypernetwork_adapter existing comment).\n\n6. Source layout: all code stays under\n   libs/model-training/src/model_training/\n\n7. Environment variables already in use:\n     RUNE_BASE_MODEL              → base model ID (default Qwen/Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct)\n     RUNE_ADAPTER_DIR             → adapter output directory\n     RUNE_DATABASE_URL            → SQLAlchemy DB URL\n     RUNE_HYPERNETWORK_WEIGHTS_PATH → path to hypernetwork checkpoint\n   Add:\n     RUNE_HIDDEN_LAYER_INDEX      → int, default -1 (last layer)\n\n━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\nTRAINING SCRIPT\n━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\nCreate a new file:\n  libs/model-training/src/model_training/train_d2l_qwen3.py\n\nThis script must:\n1. Accept CLI args (use argparse, no new deps):\n     --base-model       str   default=RUNE_BASE_MODEL env or \"Qwen/Qwen3-Coder-Next\"\n     --checkpoint       str   default=RUNE_HYPERNETWORK_WEIGHTS_PATH env\n     --output-dir       str   required\n     --data-path        str   required  (JSONL file: {doc, queries, answers})\n     --rank             int   default=8\n     --lora-targets     str   nargs=\"+\" default=[\"q_proj\", \"v_proj\"]\n     --epochs           int   default=3\n     --lr               float default=1e-4\n     --batch-size       int   default=2\n     --grad-accum       int   default=8\n     --max-doc-len      int   default=2048\n     --bf16             flag  default=True\n     --flash-attn       flag  default=True\n     --dry-run          flag  (shape-check + 1 batch, then exit)\n     --smoke-test       flag  (5 steps, tiny data subset)\n\n2. Training loop:\n   a. Load frozen Qwen3-Coder-Next with device_map=\"auto\", torch_dtype=bfloat16.\n      Freeze all parameters (requires_grad=False).\n   b. Discover attention layer indices dynamically (see TARGET MODEL step 5).\n      These are the layers where LoRA adapters will be injected.\n   c. Load HyperLoRA from qwen_4b_d2l checkpoint via load_sakana_checkpoint().\n      Apply partial transfer strategy (freeze aggregator, re-init head).\n      Only retrained components have requires_grad=True.\n   d. Data loading: read JSONL where each line is\n        {\"doc\": \"...\", \"queries\": [\"q1\",\"q2\",...], \"answers\": [\"a1\",\"a2\",...]}\n      For each (doc, query, answer) triple, this is one training example.\n      NOTE: This is a NEW format for D2L distillation training, separate\n      from the trajectory format used by QLoRA training elsewhere in rune.\n   e. Teacher forward pass: Qwen3-Coder-Next with doc+query in context →\n      extract logits and hidden states (for distillation target and\n      perceiver input respectively).\n      Use extract_activations_with_model() (the new function that accepts\n      a pre-loaded model, avoiding reload per step).\n   f. Hypernetwork forward: activations → HyperLoRA perceiver →\n      generate_weights() → LoRA adapter weights.\n   g. Student forward pass: inject generated LoRA adapter into Qwen3-Coder-Next\n      using inject_lora_adapter() (see below) →\n      run with query only (no doc) → get student logits.\n   h. Loss: KL divergence between teacher logits and student logits,\n      computed over the answer token positions only.\n      Also log: teacher_loss (NLL on answer), student_loss (NLL on answer),\n      kl_loss, adapter_gen_latency_ms, gpu_mem_gb.\n   i. Backprop through hypernetwork only (frozen parts have no grad).\n   j. AdamW optimizer, cosine LR schedule with warmup (warmup_ratio=0.05).\n   k. Save checkpoint every 500 steps and at end:\n        torch.save({\n            \"model_state_dict\": hypernet.state_dict(),\n            \"hypernet_config\": hc,\n            \"step\": step,\n            \"base_model_id\": BASE_MODEL_ID,\n            \"attention_layer_indices\": attn_layer_indices,\n        }, output_dir/checkpoint_{step}.pt)\n\n3. --dry-run mode:\n   - Load models, build one batch, run one forward pass.\n   - Print all tensor shapes: activations, perceiver output,\n     generated LoRA weights per layer, student logits, KL loss value.\n   - Print discovered attention layer indices for the hybrid model.\n   - Assert all shapes match expectations.\n   - Exit 0 on success.\n\n4. Student LoRA injection helper:\n     def inject_lora_adapter(\n         base_model,\n         adapter_state_dict: dict[str, torch.Tensor],\n         target_modules: list[str],\n         rank: int,\n         alpha: int,\n     ) -> context_manager:\n         ...\n   It must use PEFT's get_peft_model with a LoraConfig matching\n   the generated adapter, load the state_dict, then yield the model,\n   and on exit call peft_model.delete_adapter(\"default\") to restore\n   the base model to its original state (no side effects between\n   training steps). This avoids repeated model reload overhead.\n   Alternative: use peft_model.merge_and_unload() on exit, though\n   delete_adapter is cheaper for repeated injection cycles.\n\n━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\nDATA FORMAT AND SYNTHETIC DATA GENERATOR\n━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\nCreate:\n  libs/model-training/src/model_training/d2l_data.py\n\nNOTE: This data format is specific to Doc-to-LoRA distillation training.\nIt is separate from the trajectory format used by the QLoRA training\npipeline and the hash-based DocToLoraHypernetwork. Each record pairs a\ndocument with comprehension queries — the model learns to distill the\ndocument's knowledge into a LoRA adapter.\n\nFunctions:\n  generate_needle_dataset(\n      n_docs: int = 100,\n      n_queries_per_doc: int = 5,\n      doc_length_tokens: int = 1024,\n      seed: int = 42,\n  ) -> list[dict]:\n      \"\"\"Needle-in-haystack: insert a random 8-digit code into filler text.\n      Query asks 'what is the secret code?', answer is the code.\"\"\"\n\n  generate_code_doc_dataset(\n      hf_dataset_id: str = \"codeparrot/github-code\",\n      n_docs: int = 500,\n      n_queries_per_doc: int = 3,\n  ) -> list[dict]:\n      \"\"\"Load real code files, generate comprehension queries with answers.\"\"\"\n\n  save_jsonl(data: list[dict], path: str) -> None\n  load_jsonl(path: str) -> list[dict]\n\n━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\nCONFIG UPDATE\n━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\nIn libs/model-training/src/model_training/config.py, add:\n\n  def get_d2l_qwen3_config(\n      base_model_id: str = \"Qwen/Qwen3-Coder-Next\",\n      rank: int = 8,\n      target_modules: list[str] | None = None,\n  ) -> dict:\n      \"\"\"Return HyperLoRA config kwargs targeting Qwen3-Coder-Next.\n\n      Reads hidden_size, num_hidden_layers from model config via AutoConfig.\n      Discovers attention layer indices dynamically for the hybrid\n      DeltaNet + MoE architecture.\n\n      Does NOT hard-code latent_dim, depth, or heads — these are read\n      from the source checkpoint's hypernet_config at load time.\n\n      If target_modules is None, defaults to [\"q_proj\", \"v_proj\"]\n      (attention projections on the 12 attention layers).\n      \"\"\"\n\n━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\nTESTS\n━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\nAdd/extend tests in libs/model-training/tests/:\n\ntest_d2l_training.py:\n  - test_extract_activations_with_model_shape: mock base model,\n    confirm output shape is (1, num_layers, seq_len, hidden_dim).\n  - test_aggregator_frozen_after_partial_load: load checkpoint, confirm\n    aggregator.* parameters have requires_grad=False.\n  - test_head_retrained_has_grad: confirm head.* parameters\n    have requires_grad=True after re-initialization.\n  - test_adapter_key_format: call generate_weights(), confirm all keys\n    match valid PEFT LoRA key patterns for the discovered layer indices.\n  - test_adapter_shapes: confirm lora_A is (rank, in_features)\n    and lora_B is (out_features, rank) for each target layer.\n  - test_cpu_importable: import model_training.sakana_d2l without torch\n    available (mock torch to None), confirm no ImportError.\n  - test_dry_run_shapes: run extract_activations_with_model with a\n    small mock model, full HyperLoRA forward, check all shapes,\n    assert KL loss is finite.\n  - test_inject_lora_context_manager: inject adapter, confirm model has\n    LoRA layers; exit context, confirm model reverted.\n  - test_attention_layer_discovery: mock a hybrid model config, confirm\n    only attention layers (not DeltaNet layers) are returned.\n\ntest_d2l_data.py:\n  - test_needle_dataset_format\n  - test_needle_answer_recoverable\n  - test_jsonl_round_trip\n\n━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\nDELIVERABLES (in order)\n━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n1. Qwen3-Coder-Next architecture probe\n   → print hidden_size, num_hidden_layers, intermediate_size,\n     num_attention_heads, num_key_value_heads, model_type,\n     num_experts, and computed GQA dims.\n   → discover and print attention layer indices (non-DeltaNet layers).\n\n2. Partial weight transfer implementation plan\n   → list exactly which HyperLoRA state_dict keys (aggregator.*, head.*)\n     are copied vs re-initialized.\n\n3. Extensions to sakana_d2l.py  (unified diff or full file)\n\n4. New train_d2l_qwen3.py  (full file)\n\n5. New d2l_data.py  (full file)\n\n6. Updated config.py  (unified diff or full file)\n\n7. Updated pyproject.toml if new deps are needed\n   (add under [project.optional-dependencies] gpu = [...])\n   Note: datasets is already in root pyproject.toml main dependencies.\n\n8. New test files  (full files)\n\n9. Shell commands (all use uv run):\n   a. Create environment and install:\n        uv sync --all-packages --extra gpu\n   b. Generate smoke-test dataset (needle, 20 docs):\n        uv run python -m model_training.d2l_data --needle --n-docs 20 --out /tmp/needle.jsonl\n   c. Architecture probe:\n        uv run python -c \"from model_training.train_d2l_qwen3 import probe_model_config; probe_model_config()\"\n   d. Dry run (shape check):\n        uv run python -m model_training.train_d2l_qwen3 \\\n          --base-model Qwen/Qwen3-Coder-Next \\\n          --checkpoint $RUNE_HYPERNETWORK_WEIGHTS_PATH \\\n          --output-dir /tmp/d2l_dry \\\n          --data-path /tmp/needle.jsonl \\\n          --dry-run\n   e. Smoke-test training (5 steps):\n        uv run python -m model_training.train_d2l_qwen3 \\\n          --base-model Qwen/Qwen3-Coder-Next \\\n          --checkpoint $RUNE_HYPERNETWORK_WEIGHTS_PATH \\\n          --output-dir /tmp/d2l_smoke \\\n          --data-path /tmp/needle.jsonl \\\n          --smoke-test\n   f. Full training launch:\n        uv run python -m model_training.train_d2l_qwen3 \\\n          --base-model Qwen/Qwen3-Coder-Next \\\n          --checkpoint $RUNE_HYPERNETWORK_WEIGHTS_PATH \\\n          --output-dir $RUNE_ADAPTER_DIR/d2l_qwen3 \\\n          --data-path /path/to/full_train.jsonl \\\n          --epochs 3 --rank 8 --lora-targets q_proj v_proj \\\n          --bf16 --flash-attn --batch-size 2 --grad-accum 8\n   g. Run tests:\n        cd libs/model-training && uv run pytest tests/ -v\n\n10. \"Assumptions and blockers\" section covering:\n    - Qwen3-Coder-Next availability on HuggingFace at run time\n    - Hybrid DeltaNet + MoE architecture: only 12 of 48 layers\n      have standard attention. LoRA targets MUST be discovered\n      dynamically — never hard-code layer indices.\n    - Whether qwen_4b_d2l checkpoint's latent_size is compatible.\n      Read from hypernet_config at load time — if it differs from\n      expectations, raise a clear error with both expected and actual.\n    - Student LoRA injection overhead per step and mitigation:\n      use delete_adapter(\"default\") instead of full model reload.\n    - MoE routing: hidden state extraction must handle expert-routed\n      layers. The shared expert's activations may be more stable\n      for distillation than the per-token routed expert activations.\n\n━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\nCONSTRAINTS\n━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n- Never import torch at module level (INFRA-05).\n- Never hard-code hidden_size, num_layers, intermediate_size, or\n  attention layer indices — always read from cfg or discover dynamically.\n- Never hard-code latent_size, depth, or heads — read from checkpoint config.\n- Never copy perceiver weights between mismatched latent_dim values\n  without raising a clear error.\n- Never target mlp.down_proj on MoE models without explicitly handling\n  per-expert routing (prefer attention projections instead).\n- Never add modules_to_save to any LoraConfig or adapter_config.json.\n- All new public functions must have full type annotations and docstrings.\n- Keep all code inside libs/model-training/src/model_training/ unless\n  adding a top-level script that imports from there.\n- Do not introduce new top-level dependencies outside\n  [project.optional-dependencies] gpu.\n- Do not modify DocToLoraHypernetwork in hypernetwork.py — it remains\n  unchanged for the hash-based fast adapter generation path.\n"
}
